===== torque-trail/NOTES.md =====
     1	Torque Trail: drive the gold truck with the left vertical throttle/brake and right drag steering.
     2	Keyboard: WASD or arrow keys; W/Space/Enter engages a nearby winch when bogged; R recovers; reset is instant.
     3	Tap a settlement, choose a job, then deliver cargo to the glowing destination settlement.
     4	Pick ROAD, MUD, or ROCK tires at any settlement; each has honest surface-specific grip.
     5	Cash, position, current cargo, jobs, and best cash save automatically in localStorage.

===== vertol-rescue/NOTES.md =====
     1	# Vertol Rescue
     2	
     3	Controls: drag the left stick to fly; drag the right WINCH lever down to lower and up to raise. Keyboard: WASD fly, Arrow Up/Down winch, Space/Enter restart.
     4	
     5	Loop: hook all 8 stranded survivors, lift them aboard, and land at the hospital pad before fuel or hull runs out. Best score is saved locally.

===== redline-gt/NOTES.md =====
     1	Controls: drag left/right on the road to steer; hold ACCEL or BRAKE.
     2	Keyboard: arrows or WASD; Space accelerates; R restarts the race.
     3	Race: drive 3 laps, chase your best-lap ghost marker, then pick another circuit.
     4	Six original seeded circuits grow more technical from Copper Halo to Obsidian Crown.
     5	Best laps and best total times persist in localStorage on this device.

===== cloudhopper/NOTES.md =====
     1	Cloudhopper is a low-poly free-flight sortie.
     2	Drag the left virtual stick: down pulls the nose up; left/right banks.
     3	Slide the right-edge POWER control, or use W/S for throttle and arrows to fly.
     4	Pass all 8 rings, then make a gentle landing on the pale strip.
     5	Crash or land, then tap or press Space/Enter to restart; best time is saved locally.

===== dune-runner/NOTES.md =====
     1	# Dune Runner
     2	
     3	Controls: drag the left wheel or anywhere on the left half to steer; hold THROTTLE to accelerate; BRAKE slows you down; arrows/WASD also work.
     4	Loop: follow the compass, tag every gold flag in a checkpoint raid before its 60-second timer, and refuel only while coasting inside an oasis.
     5	Fuel depletion triggers a walk-back to the nearest oasis for a 25-point penalty. Five raids clear the route; END RUN banks the session score.
     6	Best score is saved in localStorage. Portrait mode shows a rotate prompt; the game runs underneath it.
===== dune-runner/index.html =====
     1	<!doctype html>
     2	<html lang="en">

     7	  <title>Dune Runner</title>

===== driftlands/NOTES.md =====
     1	Driftlands is a seeded island adventure with three hidden five-room gauntlets and a final ruin boss.
     2	Move with the left stick or WASD / arrow keys; press ACTION or Space / Enter to slash, open, and enter.
     3	Find relic chests to raise sword power, max hearts, and movement speed, then return to the sealed ruin.
     4	Enemies can drop hearts; death respawns at camp with gear intact, and progress autosaves in localStorage.
     5	The minimap reveals as you roam; defeat the tide-heart to win and use the end-screen button to restart.

===== deep-ballast/NOTES.md =====
     1	# Deep Ballast
     2	
     3	Controls (touch): hold BALLAST to flood tanks and sink, release to blow tanks and rise; drag anywhere else left/right to steer; tap SONAR to ping.
     4	Controls (keyboard): Space / Down / S = ballast, Arrows or A/D = steer, E / Enter / Shift = sonar ping.
     5	Loop: pings are your only eyes in the dark trench, but big fauna swim toward the last ping. Grab 3 salvage crates and surface before air runs out or pressure below the redline crushes the hull.
     6	Salvage banks only when you surface alive; spend it in DRY DOCK on air tanks (longer dives) and hull plating (deeper redline).
     7	Best depth, bank and upgrades persist in localStorage.

===== tide-harbor/NOTES.md =====
     1	Controls: tap water to set a course; drag the TRIM ring to angle the sail.
     2	Keyboard: arrows steer/trim; WASD also steers/trims; E docks, R restarts.
     3	Loop: sail between docks, buy low, sell high, and upgrade the hull with gold.
     4	Storm cells give a speed boost but can spill cargo.
     5	Goals: reach 1,000 gold, 5,000 gold, and the flagship hull; progress autosaves.

===== rally-dust/NOTES.md =====
     1	# Rally Dust
     2	
     3	Controls: drag left/right on the left half to steer; hold HANDBRAKE or Space to rotate; BRAKE or Down/S to slow.
     4	Keyboard: Arrow keys or WASD steer/brake; Space/Enter handbrake.
     5	Loop: run five seeded dirt stages, follow honest two-corner-ahead pace notes, and beat the 90-second clock.
     6	Off-road tree hits trigger a three-second reset; stage times add to a rally total with medals.
     7	Best stage times and best rally total persist in localStorage.

===== shout-it/NOTES.md =====
     1	# Shout It!
     2	
     3	Loop: two teams pass one phone. Describe the on-screen phrase without saying its words; teammates guess.
     4	Controls: tap GOT IT (score a phrase point, then hand the phone to the other team) or PASS (skip, no point, you keep it).
     5	A hidden 45-75s clock ticks faster and faster, then buzzes — whoever is holding the phone loses that round point.
     6	Keyboard: SPACE / ENTER / RIGHT / D / W = GOT IT, LEFT / A / S / P / BACKSPACE = PASS, R = restart, M = mute; taps advance all other screens.
     7	First team to 7 round points wins; the win screen lists every round and offers an instant rematch. Best team phrase-run persists in localStorage.

   118	  function pointSegmentDistance(p, a, b) {
   119	    const dx = b[0] - a[0], dy = b[1] - a[1], len = dx * dx + dy * dy;

   161	  }
   162	  function drawJobBeacons() {
   163	    for (const s of settlements) { const active = state.currentJob && state.currentJob.to === settlements.indexOf(s); ctx.strokeStyle = active ?    165	  function drawVehicle() {

   169	  function drawParticles() { for (const p of particles) { const a = clamp(p.life / p.max, 0, 1); ctx.globalAlpha = a; ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.size * (1.2 - a * .3), 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1; }

   171	  function update(dt) {
   172	    dt = Math.min(dt, .05); saveClock += dt; if (saveClock > 1.2) { saveState(); saveClock = 0; }
   173	    if (toastClock > 0 && (toastClock -= dt) <= 0) ui.toast.classList.remove('show');
   174	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 19); flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 3);
   175	    const throttle = clamp(keys.ArrowUp || keys.w ? 1 : keys.ArrowDown || keys.s ? -1 : touch.throttle, -1, 1);
   176	    const steer = clamp(keys.ArrowRight || keys.d ? 1 : keys.ArrowLeft || keys.a ? -1 : touch.steer, -1, 1);
   177	    const p = state.player, surface = surfaceAt(p.x, p.y), grip = traction(surface);
   178	    if (!state.winch) {
   179	      const maxForward = surface === 'water' ? 90 : surface === 'mud' ? 105 : surface === 'rock' ? 145 : 215;
   180	      if (Math.abs(throttle) > .02) p.speed += throttle * 195 * grip * dt;
   181	      p.speed *= Math.pow(surface === 'mud' ? .21 : surface === 'water' ? .55 : surface === 'rock' ? .72 : .82, dt);
   182	      p.speed = clamp(p.speed, -105 * grip, maxForward * grip);
   183	      const steeringPower = (.65 + Math.min(1.1, Math.abs(p.speed) / 120)) * (p.speed >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
   184	      p.angle += steer * steeringPower * dt;
   185	      const oldX = p.x, oldY = p.y; p.x += Math.cos(p.angle) * p.speed * dt; p.y += Math.sin(p.angle) * p.speed * dt; p.x = clamp(p.x, 34, WORLD - 34); p.y = clamp(p.y, 34, WORLD - 34);
   186	      if (Math.abs(p.speed) > 34 && Math.random() < dt * 12) emitDust(p.x - Math.cos(p.angle) * 22, p.y - Math.sin(p.angle) * 22, surface);
   187	      if (surface === 'mud' && Math.abs(p.speed) < 28 && throttle > .25) state.bogTimer += dt * (1.3 / Math.max(.5, grip)); else state.bogTimer = Math.max(0, state.bogTimer - dt * 1.6);
   188	      if (state.bogTimer > 2.5) { state.bogged = true; p.speed *= .84; }
   189	      if (state.bogged && surface !== 'mud') { state.bogged = false; state.bogTimer = 0; }
   190	      if (oldX !== p.x || oldY !== p.y) { /* movement is intentionally plain for a forgiving prototype */ }
   191	    } else {
   192	      const a = state.winch.anchor, dx = a.x - p.x, dy = a.y - p.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy); p.speed = 0;
   193	      if (d > 35) { p.x += dx / d * 168 * dt; p.y += dy / d * 168 * dt; p.angle = Math.atan2(dy, dx); if (Math.random() < dt * 15) emitDust(p.x, p.y, 'mud'); }
   194	      state.winch.time -= dt; if (d <= 38 || state.winch.time <= 0) { state.winch = null; state.bogged = false; state.bogTimer = 0; toast('Winch bite! Back on the trail.'); flash = 1; }
   195	    }
   196	    const ns = nearestSettlement(); if (ns) state.lastSettlement = settlements.indexOf(ns);
   197	    if (state.currentJob && dist(p, settlements[state.currentJob.to]) < 90 && !state.currentJob.delivered) completeJob();
   198	    const target = { x: p.x, y: p.y }; camera.x = lerp(camera.x, clamp(target.x, viewW / zoom / 2, WORLD - viewW / zoom / 2), 1 - Math.pow(.001, dt)); camera.y = lerp(camera.y, clamp(target.y, viewH / zoom / 2, WORLD - viewH / zoom / 2), 1 - Math.pow(.001, dt));
   199	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const q = particles[i]; q.life -= dt; q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt; q.vx *= Math.pow(.04, dt); q.vy *= Math.pow(.04, dt); if (q.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1); }
   200	    updateUI(); draw();
   201	  }
   202	  function emitDust(x, y, surface) { const color = surface === 'mud' ? '#7d5d42' : surface === 'rock' ? '#aa9d82' : '#bd9762'; particles.push({ x, y, vx: (Math.random() - .5) * 42, vy: (Math.random() - .5) * 42, size: 3 + Math.random() * 5, life: .4 + Math.random() * .35, max: .75, color }); if (particles.length > 100) particles.shift(); }

   204	  function updateUI() {
   205	    ui.cash.textContent = money(state.cash); ui.jobs.textContent = state.jobs + (state.jobs === 1 ? ' run' : ' runs'); ui.tire.textContent = tireStats[state.tire].label;
   206	    if (state.currentJob) { const j = jobs[state.currentJob.jobIndex], from = settlements[j.from], to = settlements[j.to]; ui.objective.style.display = 'block'; ui.objective.textContent = `${j.cargo.toUpperCase()} → ${to.name} · ${money(j.pay)}`; ui.tip.textContent = `Deliver the cargo to ${to.name}.`; } else { ui.objective.style.display = 'none'; ui.tip.textContent = state.bogged ? 'Bogged — find a gold-ringed tree or recover.' : 'Tap a settlement to browse delivery work.'; }
   207	    const anchor = nearestAnchor(); ui.winch.style.display = anchor && state.bogged && !state.winch ? 'block' : 'none'; const stalled = state.bogged && !anchor && state.bogTimer > 3.4 && !state.winch; ui.recover.style.display = stalled ? 'block' : 'none'; ui.restart.style.display = stalled ? 'block' : 'none';
   208	  }
   209	  function openSettlement(index) {

   211	    jobs.forEach((j, index2) => { if (j.from !== index) return; const to = settlements[j.to], ready = near && !state.currentJob; const card = document.createElement('button'); card.className = 'job' + (ready ?    212	    document.querySelectorAll('.tire').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('selected', b.dataset.tire === state.tire)); ui.shade.style.display = 'flex';

   214	  function closePanel() { ui.shade.style.display = 'none'; panelSettlement = null; }
   215	  function takeJob(jobIndex) { const j = jobs[jobIndex]; if (state.currentJob || dist(state.player, settlements[j.from]) > 125) return; state.currentJob = { jobIndex, from: j.from, to: j.to }; saveState(); closePanel(); toast(`Loaded ${j.cargo}.    216	  function completeJob() { const j = jobs[state.currentJob.jobIndex]; state.cash += j.pay; state.jobs++; state.bestCash = Math.max(state.bestCash, state.cash); state.currentJob = null; state.bogged = false; state.bogTimer = 0; saveState(); shake = 8; flash = 1; toast(`Delivered! +${money(j.pay)} · ${state.jobs} runs complete`, 3.4); for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) emitDust(state.player.x, state.player.y, 'road'); }
   217	  function deployWinch() { const anchor = nearestAnchor(); if (!anchor || !state.bogged || state.winch) return; state.winch = { anchor, time: 2.4 }; toast('Winch engaged — hold the line.'); shake = 3; }
   218	  function recover() { if (!state.bogged || nearestAnchor()) return; const cost = Math.min(25, state.cash); state.cash -= cost; const s = settlements[state.lastSettlement] || settlements[0]; state.player.x = s.x + 64; state.player.y = s.y + 50; state.player.speed = 0; state.bogged = false; state.bogTimer = 0; saveState(); shake = 11; flash = 1; toast(cost ? `Recovered at ${s.name} · -${money(cost)}` : `Recovered at ${s.name} · empty wallet`, 3); }
   219	  function restartTrip() { const s = settlements[state.lastSettlement] || settlements[0]; state.currentJob = null; state.player.x = s.x + 64; state.player.y = s.y + 50; state.player.speed = 0; state.bogged = false; state.bogTimer = 0; saveState(); shake = 8; flash = 1; toast(`Trip reset at ${s.name}.`, 2.5); }

   132	    const r = Math.min(radius, Math.abs(w) * 0.5, Math.abs(h) * 0.5);

   177	    return { x: vw - Math.min(86, vw * 0.12), top, bottom };
   178	  }
   179	  function setStick(clientX, clientY) {
   180	    const base = getStickBase();
   181	    const dx = clientX - base.x;
   182	    const dy = clientY - base.y;
   183	    const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   184	    const radius = base.radius;
   185	    const scale = Math.min(1, radius / distance);
   186	    input.stickX = clamp((dx * scale) / radius, -1, 1);
   187	    input.stickY = clamp((-dy * scale) / radius, -1, 1);
   188	  }
   189	  function setLever(clientY) {
   190	    const bounds = getLeverBounds();
   191	    input.lever = clamp((clientY - bounds.top) / (bounds.bottom - bounds.top), 0, 1);
   192	    state.cableTarget = 42 + input.lever * 182;
   193	  }
   194	
   195	  function pointerDown(event) {
   196	    event.preventDefault();
   197	    if (state.mode !== 'playing') {
   198	      resetGame();
   199	      return;
   200	    }
   201	    const x = event.clientX;
   202	    const y = event.clientY;
   203	    if (x < vw * 0.46 && y > vh * 0.48) {
   204	      input.stickId = event.pointerId;
   205	      setStick(x, y);
   206	      canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   207	    } else if (x > vw * 0.66) {
   208	      input.leverId = event.pointerId;
   209	      setLever(y);
   210	      canvas.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   211	    }
   212	  }
   213	  function pointerMove(event) {
   214	    event.preventDefault();
   215	    if (event.pointerId === input.stickId) setStick(event.clientX, event.clientY);
   216	    if (event.pointerId === input.leverId) setLever(event.clientY);
   217	  }
   218	  function pointerUp(event) {
   219	    event.preventDefault();
   220	    if (event.pointerId === input.stickId) {
   221	      input.stickId = null;
   222	      input.stickX = 0;
   223	      input.stickY = 0;
   224	    }
   225	    if (event.pointerId === input.leverId) input.leverId = null;
   226	  }
   227	
   228	  function keyChange(event, down) {
   229	    const code = event.code;
   230	    if (['KeyW', 'KeyA', 'KeyS', 'KeyD', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Space', 'Enter', 'KeyR'].includes(code)) {
   231	      event.preventDefault();
   232	    }
   233	    if (down && (code === 'Space' || code === 'Enter' || code === 'KeyR') && state.mode !== 'playing') {
   234	      resetGame();
   235	      return;
   236	    }
   237	    if (down) input.keys.add(code); else input.keys.delete(code);
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  function keyDown(code) { return input.keys.has(code); }
   241	  function horizontalInput() {
   242	    return clamp(input.stickX + (keyDown('KeyD') ? 1 : 0) - (keyDown('KeyA') ? 1 : 0), -1, 1);
   243	  }
   244	  function verticalInput() {
   245	    return clamp(input.stickY + (keyDown('KeyW') ? 1 : 0) - (keyDown('KeyS') ? 1 : 0), -1, 1);
   246	  }
   247	  function adjustWinch(dt) {
   248	    if (keyDown('ArrowDown')) state.cableTarget += 180 * dt;
   249	    if (keyDown('ArrowUp')) state.cableTarget -= 180 * dt;
   250	    state.cableTarget = clamp(state.cableTarget, 42, 224);
   251	    input.lever = (state.cableTarget - 42) / 182;
   252	  }

   634	
   635	  function drawParticles() {
   636	    for (const particle of state.particles) {
   637	      ctx.globalAlpha = clamp(particle.life / particle.maxLife, 0, 1);
   638	      ctx.fillStyle = particle.color;
   639	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(particle.x, particle.y, particle.size, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   640	    }
   641	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   642	  }
   643	
   644	  function drawWindsock() {
   645	    const x = 324;
   646	    const y = 72;
   647	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#9fbcbf';
   648	    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   649	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y + 38); ctx.lineTo(x, y); ctx.stroke();
   650	    const direction = state.wind >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
   651	    const strength = clamp(Math.abs(state.wind) / 58, 0.16, 1);
   652	    ctx.fillStyle = Math.abs(state.wind) > 30 ? '#ff9f73' : '#ffcb78';
   653	    ctx.beginPath();
   654	    ctx.moveTo(x, y + 2);
   655	    ctx.lineTo(x + direction * (37 + 36 * strength), y + 8);
   656	    ctx.lineTo(x + direction * (31 + 29 * strength), y + 20);
   657	    ctx.lineTo(x, y + 18);
   658	    ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   659	    text(Math.abs(state.wind) > 30 ? 'GUST' : 'WIND', x, y + 54, 10, Math.abs(state.wind) > 30 ? '#ffb48a' : '#b5ded9', 'center', 800);
   660	  }

   745	
   746	  function drawPortraitOverlay() {
   747	    if (vw >= vh * 0.96) return;
   748	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(5, 11, 20, .84)';
   749	    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, vw, vh);
   750	    const cx = vw * 0.5;
   751	    const cy = vh * 0.5;
   752	    ctx.save();
   753	    ctx.translate(cx - 28, cy - 40);
   754	    ctx.rotate(-0.35);
   755	    ctx.strokeStyle = '#73f2ba';
   756	    ctx.lineWidth = 5;
   757	    rr(-19, -34, 38, 68, 8); ctx.stroke();
   758	    ctx.fillStyle = '#73f2ba'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 25, 3, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   759	    ctx.restore();
   760	    text('ROTATE YOUR PHONE', cx + 24, cy - 16, 17, '#f6f4df', 'center', 900);
   761	    text('LANDSCAPE FLIGHT DECK', cx + 24, cy + 14, 10, '#9eb4bd', 'center', 800);
   762	  }
   763	
   764	  function render() {
   765	    ctx.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);
   766	    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, vw, vh);
   767	    drawBackground();
   768	    const shake = state.cameraShake > 0 ? state.cameraShake * 8 : 0;
   769	    ctx.save();
   770	    ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * shake, (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake);
   771	    drawWorld();
   772	    ctx.restore();
   773	    drawHUD();
   774	    drawStateCard();
   775	    drawPortraitOverlay();
   776	  }
   777	
   778	  function frame(now) {
   779	    const dt = Math.min(0.034, Math.max(0.001, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   780	    lastTime = now;
   781	    update(dt);
   782	    render();
   783	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   784	  }
   785	
   786	  resize();
   787	  resetGame();
   788	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize, { passive: true });
   789	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   790	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   791	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   792	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   793	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   794	  window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => keyChange(event, true), { passive: false });
   795	  window.addEventListener('keyup', (event) => keyChange(event, false), { passive: false });
   796	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   797	})();

    55	  function random(seed) {

    63	  function makeTrack(cfg) {
    64	    var rng = random(cfg.seed);
    65	    var count = 320 + cfg.difficulty * 28;
    66	    var segments = [];
    67	    var curve = 0, hill = 0, worldX = 0;
    68	    var targetCurve = 0, targetHill = 0;
    69	    var block = 0;
    70	    for (var i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    71	      if (block-- <= 0) {
    72	        block = 12 + Math.floor(rng() * (22 - cfg.difficulty));
    73	        var turn = (rng() * 2 - 1) * (0.42 + cfg.difficulty * 0.075);
    74	        if (rng() < 0.18) turn *= 0.4;
    75	        targetCurve = turn;
    76	        targetHill = (rng() * 2 - 1) * (28 + cfg.difficulty * 11);
    77	      }
    78	      curve += (targetCurve - curve) * (0.09 + cfg.difficulty * 0.003);
    79	      hill += (targetHill - hill) * 0.07;
    80	      if (i % 47 === 12) hill += (rng() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * (24 + cfg.difficulty * 3);
    81	      worldX += curve * 0.028;
    82	      var obstacle = null;
    83	      if (i > 20 && i < count - 15 && rng() < 0.025 + cfg.difficulty * 0.006) {
    84	        obstacle = { side: rng() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1, x: (rng() * 0.28 + 0.67) * (rng() > 0.5 ? 1 : -1), kind: rng() > 0.5 ? 'post' : 'rock' };
    85	      }
    86	      segments.push({ curve: curve, hill: hill, worldX: worldX, obstacle: obstacle, stripe: i % 2 });
    87	    }
    88	    return { config: cfg, segments: segments, length: count * SEG_LEN };
    89	  }

   166	  function steerValue() {
   167	    var value = pointer.drag ? pointer.steer : 0;
   168	    if (keys.left) value -= 1;
   169	    if (keys.right) value += 1;
   170	    return clamp(value, -1, 1);
   171	  }
   172	  function accelHeld() { return pointer.accel || keys.accel; }
   173	  function brakeHeld() { return pointer.brake || keys.brake; }

   175	  function update(dt) {
   176	    updateParticles(dt);
   177	    flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt);
   178	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt);
   179	    if (state !== 'race' || portrait()) return;
   180	    hintTime = Math.max(0, hintTime - dt);
   181	    game.messageTime = Math.max(0, game.messageTime - dt);
   182	    var cfg = track.config;
   183	    var maxSpeed = 650 + cfg.difficulty * 34;
   184	    var throttle = accelHeld() ? 1 : 0.35;
   185	    if (brakeHeld()) throttle = -1;
   186	    game.speed += throttle * 570 * dt;
   187	    if (!accelHeld() && !brakeHeld()) game.speed += 105 * dt;
   188	    if (brakeHeld()) game.speed -= 260 * dt;
   189	    game.speed = clamp(game.speed, 0, maxSpeed);
   190	
   191	    var steer = steerValue();
   192	    var seg = currentSegment();
   193	    var curveDanger = Math.max(0, game.speed / maxSpeed - (0.64 - cfg.difficulty * 0.018));
   194	    var outward = seg.curve * curveDanger * (0.62 + cfg.difficulty * 0.04);
   195	    game.lateral += (steer * (0.83 + game.speed / maxSpeed * 0.85) + outward) * dt;
   196	    game.lateral *= 1 - dt * 0.72;
   197	    game.lateral = clamp(game.lateral, -1.45, 1.45);
   198	    if (Math.abs(game.lateral) > 0.94) {
   199	      game.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - dt * (1.1 + cfg.difficulty * 0.04));
   200	      if (Math.random() < dt * 18) spawnDust();
   201	    }
   202	    if (Math.abs(game.lateral) > 1.28) game.speed *= Math.max(0, 1 - dt * 1.5);
   203	
   204	    var nextIndex = currentIndex();
   205	    if (nextIndex !== game.lastSegment) {
   206	      game.lastSegment = nextIndex;
   207	      var obstacle = track.segments[nextIndex].obstacle;
   208	      if (obstacle && Math.abs(game.lateral - obstacle.x) < 0.22) hitObstacle();
   209	    }
   210	    game.speed = clamp(game.speed, 0, maxSpeed);
   211	    game.distance += game.speed * dt;
   212	    game.lapTime += dt;
   213	    game.totalTime += dt;
   214	    game.hitTimer = Math.max(0, game.hitTimer - dt);
   215	    var lapLength = track.length;
   216	    if (game.distance >= lapLength) {

   251	  function spawnDust() {
   252	    particles.push({ x: W / 2 + (game.lateral > 0 ? 1 : -1) * (W * 0.27 + Math.random() * 30), y: H * 0.88, vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 50, vy: -25 - Math.random() * 50, life: 0.5, size: 4 + Math.random() * 8, color: '#e3c090' });
   253	  }
   254	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   255	    for (var i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   256	      var p = particles[i];
   257	      p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vy += 75 * dt;
   258	      if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   259	    }
   260	  }

   262	  function finishRace() {
   263	    var total = game.totalTime;
   264	    var cfg = track.config;
   265	    var medal = total <= cfg.gold ? 'GOLD' : total <= cfg.silver ? 'SILVER' : 'BRONZE';
   266	    game.medal = medal;
   267	    if (!bests[trackIndex].total || total < bests[trackIndex].total) bests[trackIndex].total = total;
   268	    saveBests();
   269	    finishInfo = { total: total, medal: medal, lap: bests[trackIndex].lap, bestTotal: bests[trackIndex].total };
   270	    state = 'select';
   271	    flash = 0.3;
   272	  }

   274	  function draw() {
   275	    ctx.save();
   276	    if (shake > 0) ctx.translate((Math.random() - 0.5) * shake * 18, (Math.random() - 0.5) * shake * 12);
   277	    drawRaceScene();

   279	    if (portrait()) drawRotateOverlay();
   280	    ctx.restore();

   311	  function roadPoint(index, currentWorld, roadCenterShift) {
   312	    var d = index;
   313	    var p = clamp(1 - d / DRAW_DISTANCE, 0, 1);
   314	    var depth = p * p;
   315	    var seg = track.segments[(Math.floor(game.distance / SEG_LEN) + index) % track.segments.length];
   316	    var y = H * 0.385 + depth * (H * 0.615) - seg.hill * depth * 0.9;
   317	    var center = W / 2 + (seg.worldX - currentWorld) * W * 0.075 * depth + roadCenterShift * depth;
   318	    var half = lerp(10, Math.min(W * 0.57, H * 0.7), Math.pow(p, 1.7));
   319	    return { x: center, y: y, half: half, p: p, seg: seg };
   320	  }

   322	  function quad(a, b, c, d, color) {
   323	    ctx.fillStyle = color; ctx.beginPath();
   324	    ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y); ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y); ctx.lineTo(c.x, c.y); ctx.lineTo(d.x, d.y); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
   325	  }

   327	  function drawRoad(cfg) {
   328	    var current = track.segments[currentIndex()];
   329	    var centerShift = -game.lateral * Math.min(W * 0.47, H * 0.56);
   330	    var roadside = rgba(cfg.edge, 0.54);
   331	    for (var i = DRAW_DISTANCE; i > 0; i--) {
   332	      var far = roadPoint(i, current.worldX, centerShift);
   333	      var near = roadPoint(i - 1, current.worldX, centerShift);
   334	      var stripe = far.seg.stripe;
   335	      var farShoulder = far.half * 1.18, nearShoulder = near.half * 1.18;
   336	      quad({ x: far.x - farShoulder, y: far.y }, { x: far.x + farShoulder, y: far.y }, { x: near.x + nearShoulder, y: near.y }, { x: near.x - nearShoulder, y: near.y }, stripe ? rgba(cfg.edge, 0.23) : rgba('#151d25', 0.46));
   337	      quad({ x: far.x - far.half, y: far.y }, { x: far.x + far.half, y: far.y }, { x: near.x + near.half, y: near.y }, { x: near.x - near.half, y: near.y }, cfg.road);
   338	      var edgeFar = Math.max(1, far.half * 0.045), edgeNear = Math.max(2, near.half * 0.045);
   339	      quad({ x: far.x - far.half, y: far.y }, { x: far.x - far.half + edgeFar, y: far.y }, { x: near.x - near.half + edgeNear, y: near.y }, { x: near.x - near.half, y: near.y }, stripe ? cfg.accent : roadside);
   340	      quad({ x: far.x + far.half - edgeFar, y: far.y }, { x: far.x + far.half, y: far.y }, { x: near.x + near.half, y: near.y }, { x: near.x + near.half - edgeNear, y: near.y }, stripe ? cfg.accent : roadside);
   341	      if (stripe === 0 && near.p > 0.08) {
   342	        var dashFar = far.half * 0.018, dashNear = near.half * 0.018;
   343	        quad({ x: far.x - dashFar, y: far.y }, { x: far.x + dashFar, y: far.y }, { x: near.x + dashNear, y: near.y }, { x: near.x - dashNear, y: near.y }, rgba('#f9f3df', 0.64));
   344	      }
   345	      if (far.seg.obstacle && i < 50 && i > 3) drawObstacle(far, far.seg.obstacle);
   346	    }
   347	  }

   454	  function trackCardRects() {
   455	    var cols = W > 700 ? 3 : 2;
   456	    var gap = Math.max(8, W * 0.018);
   457	    var cardW = (W - gap * (cols + 1)) / cols;
   458	    var cardH = Math.min(86, H * 0.16);
   459	    var startY = H * 0.23;
   460	    var rows = Math.ceil(tracks.length / cols);
   461	    if (startY + rows * (cardH + gap) > H - 48) cardH = Math.max(60, (H - startY - 48 - gap * rows) / rows);
   462	    return tracks.map(function (_, i) {
   463	      return { x: gap + (i % cols) * (cardW + gap), y: startY + Math.floor(i / cols) * (cardH + gap), w: cardW, h: cardH };
   464	    });
   465	  }

   476	  function pointerPosition(event) {
   477	    var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
   478	    return { x: event.clientX - r.left, y: event.clientY - r.top };
   479	  }
   480	  function pedalAt(x, y) {
   481	    var restart = { x: Math.max(14, W * 0.025), y: H - 110, w: Math.max(62, W * 0.11), h: 78 };
   482	    var accel = { x: W * 0.71, y: H - 110, w: Math.max(52, W * 0.13), h: 78 };
   483	    var brake = { x: W * 0.855, y: H - 110, w: Math.max(52, W * 0.13), h: 78 };
   484	    if (x >= restart.x && x <= restart.x + restart.w && y >= restart.y && y <= restart.y + restart.h) return 'restart';
   485	    if (x >= accel.x && x <= accel.x + accel.w && y >= accel.y && y <= accel.y + accel.h) return 'accel';
   486	    if (x >= brake.x && x <= brake.x + brake.w && y >= brake.y && y <= brake.y + brake.h) return 'brake';
   487	    return '';
   488	  }
   489	  function selectAt(x, y) {
   490	    var rects = trackCardRects();
   491	    for (var i = 0; i < rects.length; i++) {
   492	      var r = rects[i];
   493	      if (x >= r.x && x <= r.x + r.w && y >= r.y && y <= r.y + r.h) { startTrack(i); return true; }
   494	    }
   495	    return false;
   496	  }
   497	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', function (event) {
   498	    event.preventDefault();
   499	    var p = pointerPosition(event);
   500	    if (portrait()) return;
   501	    if (state === 'select') { selectAt(p.x, p.y); return; }
   502	    pointer.id = event.pointerId;
   503	    var pedal = pedalAt(p.x, p.y);
   504	    if (pedal === 'restart') { startTrack(trackIndex); return; }
   505	    pointer.accel = pedal === 'accel';
   506	    pointer.brake = pedal === 'brake';
   507	    if (!pointer.accel && !pointer.brake && p.x < W * 0.7) { pointer.drag = true; pointer.startX = p.x; pointer.steer = 0; }
   508	    if (canvas.setPointerCapture) canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
   509	  }, { passive: false });
   510	  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
   511	    if (event.pointerId !== pointer.id || !pointer.drag) return;
   512	    event.preventDefault();
   513	    var p = pointerPosition(event);
   514	    pointer.steer = clamp((p.x - pointer.startX) / Math.max(75, W * 0.2), -1, 1);
   515	  }, { passive: false });
   516	  function pointerEnd(event) {
   517	    if (event.pointerId !== pointer.id) return;
   518	    pointer.id = null; pointer.drag = false; pointer.steer = 0; pointer.accel = false; pointer.brake = false;
   519	  }
   520	  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerEnd, { passive: false });
   521	  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerEnd, { passive: false });
   522	  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', function (event) { event.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   523	
   524	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
   525	    var k = event.key.toLowerCase();
   526	    if (['arrowleft', 'arrowright', 'arrowup', 'arrowdown', ' ', 'w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'r', 'enter'].indexOf(k) >= 0) event.preventDefault();
   527	    if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') keys.left = true;
   528	    if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') keys.right = true;
   529	    if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w' || k === ' ') keys.accel = true;
   530	    if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') keys.brake = true;
   531	    if (k === 'r' && state === 'race') startTrack(trackIndex);
   532	    if (k === 'enter' && state === 'select') startTrack(trackIndex);
   533	  });
   534	  window.addEventListener('keyup', function (event) {
   535	    var k = event.key.toLowerCase();
   536	    if (k === 'arrowleft' || k === 'a') keys.left = false;
   537	    if (k === 'arrowright' || k === 'd') keys.right = false;
   538	    if (k === 'arrowup' || k === 'w' || k === ' ') keys.accel = false;
   539	    if (k === 'arrowdown' || k === 's') keys.brake = false;
   540	  });
   541	
   542	  function frame(now) {
   543	    var dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0, (now - lastTime) / 1000));
   544	    lastTime = now;
   545	    update(dt);
   546	    draw();
   547	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   548	  }
   549	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);

    62	  function seededRandom(value) {
    63	    const x = Math.sin(value * 127.1 + seed * 0.000013) * 43758.5453123;
    64	    return fract(x);
    65	  }

    67	  function noise2(x, z) {
    68	    const a = seededRandom(Math.floor(x) * 0.73 + Math.floor(z) * 1.17);
    69	    const b = seededRandom(Math.ceil(x) * 0.73 + Math.ceil(z) * 1.17);
    70	    return lerp(a, b, fract(x));
    71	  }

    73	  function terrainAt(x, z) {
    74	    const ridgePhase = seed * 0.0000007;
    75	    const broad = Math.sin(z * 0.014 + ridgePhase * 11) * 2.9;
    76	    const cross = Math.sin(x * 0.035 - z * 0.008 + ridgePhase * 7) * 2.2;
    77	    const ridge = Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(z * 0.026 + x * 0.012 + ridgePhase * 19)), 7) * 8;
    78	    const rumble = (noise2(x * 0.11, z * 0.075) - .5) * 2.0;
    79	    let h = 5.3 + broad + cross + ridge + rumble;
    80	    const lakeX = -62 + Math.sin(ridgePhase * 17) * 12;
    81	    const lakeZ = 176 + Math.cos(ridgePhase * 11) * 18;
    82	    const lakeShape = ((x - lakeX) / 56) ** 2 + ((z - lakeZ) / 72) ** 2;
    83	    if (lakeShape < 1) {
    84	      const shore = clamp((1 - lakeShape) * 4, 0, 4);
    85	      return { h: 2.15 + shore * .12, water: true };
    86	    }
    87	    if (z > RUNWAY_START - 5 && z < RUNWAY_END + 5 && Math.abs(x - RUNWAY_X) < RUNWAY_WIDTH * .57) {
    88	      return { h: 3.3, runway: true };
    89	    }
    90	    return { h: Math.max(0.4, h), water: false, runway: false };
    91	  }

   187	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   188	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   189	      const particle = particles[i];
   190	      particle.life -= dt;
   191	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;

   193	      particle.vy += 42 * dt;
   194	      if (particle.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   195	    }
   196	  }

   198	  function project(x, y, z) {
   199	    const player = sortie.player;
   200	    const relativeZ = z - player.z;
   201	    if (relativeZ < 3) return null;

   204	    const focal = Math.min(width, height) * .94;
   205	    const scale = focal / relativeZ;
   206	    return {
   207	      x: width * .5 + (x - player.x) * scale,
   208	      y: horizon + (cameraY - y) * scale,
   209	      scale,
   210	      z: relativeZ,
   211	    };
   212	  }

   591	    updateParticles(dt);

   658	  function beep(frequency, duration) {
   659	    if (!audioContext) return;
   660	    try {
   661	      const oscillator = audioContext.createOscillator();
   662	      const gain = audioContext.createGain();
   663	      oscillator.frequency.value = frequency;
   664	      oscillator.type = 'triangle';
   665	      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001, audioContext.currentTime);
   666	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.045, audioContext.currentTime + .008);
   667	      gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001, audioContext.currentTime + duration);
   668	      oscillator.connect(gain).connect(audioContext.destination);
   669	      oscillator.start();
   670	      oscillator.stop(audioContext.currentTime + duration + .02);
   671	    } catch (_) { /* audio is optional */ }
   672	  }

   677	    }

   679	  }

    35	  function rng(seed) {
    36	    return () => {
    37	      seed |= 0;
    38	      seed = seed + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    39	      let t = Math.imul(seed ^ seed >>> 15, 1 | seed);
    40	      t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    41	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    42	    };
    43	  }

   116	  function resize() {
   117	    view.w = window.innerWidth;
   118	    view.h = window.innerHeight;
   119	    view.dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);

   130	  function worldToScreen(x, y) {
   131	    return { x: (x - view.camX) * view.zoom, y: (y - view.camY) * view.zoom };
   132	  }
   133	  function screenToWorld(x, y) {
   134	    return { x: x / view.zoom + view.camX, y: y / view.zoom + view.camY };
   135	  }
   136	  function terrainAt(x, y) {
   137	    let value = 0;
   138	    for (const dune of dunes) {
   139	      const dx = x - dune.x;
   140	      const dy = y - dune.y;
   141	      const ca = Math.cos(dune.angle), sa = Math.sin(dune.angle);
   142	      const rx = dx * ca + dy * sa;
   143	      const ry = -dx * sa + dy * ca;
   144	      const q = (rx * rx) / (dune.w * dune.w) + (ry * ry) / (dune.h * dune.h);
   145	      if (q < 1.7) value = Math.max(value, Math.exp(-q * 2.8) * dune.depth);
   146	    }
   147	    return value;
   148	  }
   149	  function nearestOasis() {
   150	    let found = oases[0], bestDistance = Infinity;
   151	    for (const oasis of oases) { const d = dist(player, oasis); if (d < bestDistance) { bestDistance = d; found = oasis; } }
   152	    return found;
   153	  }
   154	  function addParticle(x, y, vx, vy, life, size, color) {
   155	    if (particles.length > 230) particles.shift();
   156	    particles.push({ x, y, vx, vy, life, maxLife: life, size, color });
   157	  }
   158	  function burst(x, y, color, amount) {
   159	    for (let i = 0; i < amount; i++) {

   162	    }
   163	  }

   176	  }

   194	  function setSteerFromX(clientX) {

   200	  function startSteer(pointerId, x) {
   201	    controls.steerPointer = pointerId; controls.steerStart = x; setSteerFromX(x);

   204	    if (controls.steerPointer !== pointerId) return;

   206	  }
   207	  app.addEventListener("pointerdown", (event) => {

   222	  }

   227	  function physics(dt) {
   228	    const keyboardSteer = (keys.arrowright || keys.d ? 1 : 0) - (keys.arrowleft || keys.a ? 1 : 0);
   229	    const steer = clamp(controls.steer + keyboardSteer, -1, 1);
   230	    const throttle = controls.throttle || keys.arrowup || keys.w || keys[" "];
   231	    const brake = controls.brake || keys.arrowdown || keys.s;
   232	    const airborne = player.airborne > 0;
   233	    const oldX = player.x, oldY = player.y;
   234	    const oldTerrain = player.lastTerrain;
   235	    const terrainBefore = terrainAt(player.x, player.y);
   236	    player.lastTerrain = terrainBefore;
   237	
   238	    if (throttle && player.fuel > 0) { player.speed += 155 * dt; player.fuel -= (4.9 + player.speed / 230) * dt; }
   239	    else player.speed -= (airborne ? 4 : 24) * dt;
   240	    if (brake) player.speed -= 270 * dt;

   242	    const steeringGrip = airborne ? .82 : .72 + Math.min(.3, player.speed / 900);
   243	    player.angle += steer * (0.72 + player.speed / 180) * steeringGrip * dt;
   244	    const moveScale = airborne ? 1.04 : 1;
   245	    player.x += Math.cos(player.angle) * player.speed * dt * moveScale;
   246	    player.y += Math.sin(player.angle) * player.speed * dt * moveScale;
   247	    if (player.x < 28 || player.x > WORLD_W - 28) { player.x = clamp(player.x, 28, WORLD_W - 28); player.angle = Math.PI - player.angle; player.speed *= .68; shake = .18; }
   248	    if (player.y < 28 || player.y > WORLD_H - 28) { player.y = clamp(player.y, 28, WORLD_H - 28); player.angle = -player.angle; player.speed *= .68; shake = .18; }
   249	
   250	    const terrainAfter = terrainAt(player.x, player.y);
   251	    if (!airborne && player.speed > 180 && terrainAfter > .46 && oldTerrain <= .46) {
   252	      player.airborne = .62 + player.speed / 760; player.airHeight = 1; burst(player.x, player.y, "#e9c986", 7); toast("CREST // AIR CONTROL", 1.1);
   253	    }
   254	    player.lastTerrain = terrainAfter;
   255	    if (airborne) {
   256	      player.airborne -= dt;
   257	      player.airHeight = clamp(player.airborne * 1.8, 0, 1);
   258	      if (player.airborne <= 0) {
   259	        if (player.speed > 285) { player.speed *= .54; shake = .32; burst(player.x, player.y, "#d99453", 15); toast("HARD LANDING // SPEED LOST", 1.4); }
   260	        else { player.speed *= .88; burst(player.x, player.y, "#e9c986", 8); }
   261	        player.airHeight = 0;
   262	      }
   263	    }
   264	
   265	    player.rockCooldown -= dt;
   266	    if (!airborne && player.rockCooldown <= 0) {
   267	      for (const rock of rocks) {
   268	        if (Math.hypot(player.x - rock.x, player.y - rock.y) < rock.r + 17) {
   269	          player.rockCooldown = .38; player.speed *= .38; player.angle += (random() - .5) * 1.5; shake = .38; burst(player.x, player.y, "#b87751", 12); toast("ROCK HIT // WATCH THE RIDGES", 1.2); break;
   270	        }
   271	      }
   272	    }
   273	
   274	    for (const oasis of oases) {
   275	      if (Math.hypot(player.x - oasis.x, player.y - oasis.y) < oasis.r * .72 && !throttle && !brake) {
   276	        if (player.fuel < 99) { player.fuel = Math.min(100, player.fuel + 30 * dt); toast("REFUELING // " + oasis.name, .6); }
   277	      }
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    if (throttle && player.speed > 50 && random() < dt * 18) addParticle(player.x - Math.cos(player.angle) * 16, player.y - Math.sin(player.angle) * 16, -Math.cos(player.angle) * (25 + random() * 45), -Math.sin(player.angle) * (25 + random() * 45), .35 + random() * .28, 3 + random() * 4, "#dec182");
   281	    if (Math.hypot(player.x - oldX, player.y - oldY) > 0 && random() < dt * 5) addParticle(player.x - Math.cos(player.angle) * 14, player.y - Math.sin(player.angle) * 14, 0, 0, .28 + random() * .3, 2 + random() * 3, "#d2ac70");
   282	
   283	    if (player.fuel <= 0 && player.speed < 50) {
   284	      player.fuel = 0; state = "stranded"; strandedTime = 2.4; player.speed = 0; toast("FUEL EMPTY", 1.5);
   285	    }
   286	  }
   287	
   288	  function updateRaid(dt) {
   289	    const raid = raids[activeRaid];
   290	    raidTime -= dt;
   291	    if (raidTime <= 0) {
   292	      score = Math.max(0, score - 30); saveBest(); setRaid(activeRaid); toast("RAID MISSED // -30", 2); return;
   293	    }
   294	    for (const flag of raid.flags) {
   295	      if (!flag.tagged && Math.hypot(player.x - flag.x, player.y - flag.y) < 38) {
   296	        flag.tagged = true; burst(flag.x, flag.y, "#f2c457", 14); shake = .1; toast("FLAG TAGGED // " + (raid.flags.filter(f => f.tagged).length) + "/" + raid.flags.length, .7);
   297	      }
   298	    }
   299	    if (raid.flags.every(flag => flag.tagged)) {
   300	      const bonus = 100 + Math.ceil(raidTime * 1.6);
   301	      score += bonus; completedRaids++; saveBest(); burst(raid.x, raid.y, "#f5d36e", 32); shake = .38; toast("RAID BANKED // +" + bonus, 2.1);
   302	      if (completedRaids >= raids.length) {
   303	        state = "win";
   304	        showMessage("FIVE TARGETS CLEARED", "ROUTE CLEAR", "Every checkpoint is yours. Keep driving for a higher score, or bank this run.", "KEEP DRIVING", "END RUN", () => { state = "playing"; hideMessage(); setRaid(0); }, endSession);
   305	      } else setRaid(activeRaid + 1);
   306	    }
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  function update(dt) {
   310	    elapsed += dt;
   311	    hintTime -= dt; if (hintTime < 0) hint.classList.add("faded");
   312	    if (toastTime > 0) { toastTime -= dt; if (toastTime <= 0) statusEl.classList.remove("show"); }
   313	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const p = particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .985; p.vy *= .985; if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1); }
   314	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.8);
   315	    if (state === "playing") { physics(dt); updateRaid(dt); }
   316	    else if (state === "stranded") {
   317	      strandedTime -= dt; player.angle += dt * .5;
   318	      if (strandedTime <= 0) { const oasis = nearestOasis(); player.x = oasis.x; player.y = oasis.y; player.fuel = 100; score = Math.max(0, score - 25); saveBest(); state = "playing"; toast("WALK-BACK COMPLETE // -25", 1.8); setRaid(activeRaid); }
   319	    }
   320	    const target = raids[activeRaid];
   321	    const targetAngle = Math.atan2(target.y - player.y, target.x - player.x);
   322	    const bearing = Math.round(((targetAngle + TAU) % TAU) * 180 / Math.PI);
   323	    compassArrow.style.transform = `rotate(${bearing + 90}deg)`;
   324	    compassLabel.textContent = "TARGET // " + String(bearing).padStart(3, "0") + "°";
   325	    if (elapsed - lastUi > .1) {
   326	      scoreEl.textContent = score; bestEl.textContent = best; fuelFill.style.width = player.fuel + "%"; fuelFill.style.background = player.fuel < 24 ? "#e37a51" : player.fuel < 48 ? "#e8b356" : "#e7c66c";
   327	      const tagged = target.flags.filter(flag => flag.tagged).length;
   328	      raidReadout.textContent = "RAID " + Math.min(completedRaids + 1, 5) + "/5 · " + tagged + "/" + target.flags.length + " FLAGS · " + Math.ceil(raidTime) + "s";
   329	      lastUi = elapsed;
   330	    }
   331	  }
   332	
   333	  function drawBackground() {
   334	    const sky = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, view.h); sky.addColorStop(0, "#c8945e"); sky.addColorStop(1, "#d9b377"); ctx.fillStyle = sky; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, view.w, view.h);
   335	    ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha = .11; ctx.strokeStyle = "#f4d69a"; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   336	    const grid = 120 * view.zoom; const ox = -((view.camX * view.zoom) % grid); const oy = -((view.camY * view.zoom) % grid);
   337	    for (let x = ox; x < view.w + grid; x += grid) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, view.h); ctx.stroke(); }
   338	    for (let y = oy; y < view.h + grid; y += grid) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(view.w, y); ctx.stroke(); }
   339	    ctx.restore();
   340	  }
   341	  function visible(x, y, margin = 100) { const p = worldToScreen(x, y); return p.x > -margin && p.x < view.w + margin && p.y > -margin && p.y < view.h + margin; }
   342	  function drawDune(dune) {
   343	    if (!visible(dune.x, dune.y, dune.w)) return;
   344	    const p = worldToScreen(dune.x, dune.y); ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.rotate(dune.angle);
   345	    ctx.fillStyle = dune.tint ? "rgba(160,106,62,.27)" : "rgba(245,209,143,.42)"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 5 * view.zoom, dune.w * view.zoom, dune.h * view.zoom, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   346	    ctx.strokeStyle = dune.tint ? "rgba(123,78,50,.34)" : "rgba(255,226,165,.44)"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 2 * view.zoom); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-dune.w * .06 * view.zoom, -dune.h * .14 * view.zoom, dune.w * .82 * view.zoom, dune.h * .52 * view.zoom, 0, Math.PI * .06, Math.PI * .92); ctx.stroke();
   347	    ctx.restore();
   348	  }
   349	  function drawRock(rock) {
   350	    if (!visible(rock.x, rock.y, rock.r + 30)) return; const p = worldToScreen(rock.x, rock.y); const r = rock.r * view.zoom;
   351	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(62,48,40,.22)"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(3 * view.zoom, 6 * view.zoom, r * 1.1, r * .62, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.rotate(rock.wobble); ctx.beginPath();
   352	    for (let i = 0; i < rock.sides; i++) { const a = i / rock.sides * TAU, rr = r * (.82 + ((i * 17) % 7) / 20); const x = Math.cos(a) * rr, y = Math.sin(a) * rr * .76; if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y); } ctx.closePath(); ctx.fillStyle = rock.dark ? "#665647" : "#80654d"; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(52,45,39,.48)"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, view.zoom * 2); ctx.stroke(); ctx.restore();
   353	  }
   354	  function drawOasis(oasis) {
   355	    if (!visible(oasis.x, oasis.y, oasis.r + 60)) return; const p = worldToScreen(oasis.x, oasis.y); const r = oasis.r * view.zoom;
   356	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(78,105,67,.17)"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 6 * view.zoom, r * 1.15, r * .72, -.15, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
   357	    ctx.fillStyle = "#628f80"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 6 * view.zoom, r * .7, r * .42, -.1, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = "rgba(205,232,178,.62)"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 2 * view.zoom); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 6 * view.zoom, r * .7, r * .42, -.1, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
   358	    for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i += 2) { ctx.strokeStyle = "#4f6845"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2, view.zoom * 4); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i * r * .55, r * .28); ctx.lineTo(i * r * .62, -r * .62); ctx.stroke(); ctx.strokeStyle = "#789d62"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, view.zoom * 2); for (let j = 0; j < 3; j++) { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(i * r * .62, -r * .58 + j * 5 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(i * r * (.62 + (i * (j - 1) * .11)), -r * (.7 - j * .04)); ctx.stroke(); } }
   359	    ctx.fillStyle = "#e9d08c"; ctx.font = `800 ${Math.max(9, 11 * view.zoom)}px Trebuchet MS`; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.fillText("OASIS", 0, r * .9); ctx.restore();
   360	  }
   361	  function drawRaid(raid) {
   362	    const targetVisible = visible(raid.x, raid.y, 180); const p = worldToScreen(raid.x, raid.y); const active = raids[activeRaid] === raid;
   363	    if (targetVisible) {
   364	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x, p.y); ctx.strokeStyle = active ? "rgba(247,210,107,.5)" : "rgba(240,219,171,.16)"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 2 * view.zoom); ctx.setLineDash([6, 7]); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, raid.r * view.zoom, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]); ctx.strokeStyle = active ? "rgba(247,210,107,.36)" : "rgba(240,219,171,.12)"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, (raid.r + 18 + Math.sin(elapsed * 3) * 8) * view.zoom, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke(); if (active) { ctx.fillStyle = "#f7d26b"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, 5 * view.zoom, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); } ctx.restore();
   365	    }
   366	    if (!active) return;
   367	    for (const flag of raid.flags) {
   368	      if (flag.tagged || !visible(flag.x, flag.y, 40)) continue; const f = worldToScreen(flag.x, flag.y); const sway = Math.sin(elapsed * 4 + flag.wobble) * 2 * view.zoom; const h = 29 * view.zoom;
   369	      ctx.save(); ctx.translate(f.x, f.y); ctx.strokeStyle = "#5d493a"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 2 * view.zoom); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 9 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(0, -h); ctx.stroke(); ctx.fillStyle = "#ebbd58"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, -h); ctx.lineTo(17 * view.zoom + sway, -h + 7 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(0, -h + 14 * view.zoom); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = "#fff0a4"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 10 * view.zoom, 3 * view.zoom, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   370	    }
   371	  }
   372	  function drawOffscreenTarget() {
   373	    const target = raids[activeRaid]; const p = worldToScreen(target.x, target.y); if (p.x > 38 && p.x < view.w - 38 && p.y > 105 && p.y < view.h - 110) return;
   374	    const cx = view.w * .5, cy = view.h * .51; const dx = p.x - cx, dy = p.y - cy; const t = Math.min((view.w * .44) / Math.max(1, Math.abs(dx)), (view.h * .32) / Math.max(1, Math.abs(dy))); const x = cx + dx * t, y = cy + dy * t; const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
   375	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(x, y); ctx.rotate(a); ctx.fillStyle = "#f3ca62"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(15, 0); ctx.lineTo(-9, -9); ctx.lineTo(-5, 0); ctx.lineTo(-9, 9); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
   376	  }
   377	  function drawParticles() {
   378	    for (const p of particles) { if (!visible(p.x, p.y, 30)) continue; const s = worldToScreen(p.x, p.y); const alpha = clamp(p.life / p.maxLife, 0, 1) * .65; ctx.globalAlpha = alpha; ctx.fillStyle = p.color; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, p.size * view.zoom * (1.1 - alpha * .2), 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); } ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   379	  }
   380	  function drawPlayer() {
   381	    const p = worldToScreen(player.x, player.y); const lift = player.airHeight * 18 * view.zoom;
   382	    ctx.save(); ctx.translate(p.x + shake * (random() - .5) * 10, p.y + shake * (random() - .5) * 10 - lift); ctx.rotate(player.angle); ctx.globalAlpha = .28; ctx.fillStyle = "#40352c"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-2 * view.zoom, 11 * view.zoom + lift, 25 * view.zoom, 9 * view.zoom, 0, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   383	    ctx.fillStyle = "#2b332d"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(21 * view.zoom, 0); ctx.lineTo(7 * view.zoom, -13 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(-16 * view.zoom, -11 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(-23 * view.zoom, 0); ctx.lineTo(-16 * view.zoom, 11 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(7 * view.zoom, 13 * view.zoom); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = "#e7bd63"; ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1, 2 * view.zoom); ctx.stroke();
   384	    ctx.fillStyle = "#9bc08d"; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(9 * view.zoom, -8 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(1 * view.zoom, -6 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(1 * view.zoom, 6 * view.zoom); ctx.lineTo(9 * view.zoom, 8 * view.zoom); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill(); ctx.fillStyle = "#e5a84f"; ctx.fillRect(-13 * view.zoom, -7 * view.zoom, 7 * view.zoom, 4 * view.zoom); ctx.fillRect(-13 * view.zoom, 3 * view.zoom, 7 * view.zoom, 4 * view.zoom); ctx.restore();
   385	  }
   386	  function render() {
   387	    const follow = .08; const maxX = Math.max(0, WORLD_W - view.w / view.zoom); const maxY = Math.max(0, WORLD_H - view.h / view.zoom); view.camX = lerp(view.camX, clamp(player.x - view.w / view.zoom * .5, 0, maxX), follow); view.camY = lerp(view.camY, clamp(player.y - view.h / view.zoom * .52, 0, maxY), follow);
   388	    ctx.setTransform(view.dpr, 0, 0, view.dpr, 0, 0); drawBackground();
   389	    for (const dune of dunes) drawDune(dune); for (const oasis of oases) drawOasis(oasis); for (const rock of rocks) drawRock(rock); for (const raid of raids) drawRaid(raid); drawParticles(); drawOffscreenTarget(); drawPlayer();
   390	    if (state === "stranded") { ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(40,29,23,.18)"; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, view.w, view.h); ctx.fillStyle = "#f5e3b5"; ctx.textAlign = "center"; ctx.font = "900 24px Trebuchet MS"; ctx.fillText("STRANDED", view.w / 2, view.h * .39); ctx.font = "800 11px Trebuchet MS"; ctx.fillStyle = "#ddcda9"; ctx.fillText("WALK-BACK IN " + Math.ceil(strandedTime) + " · -25 POINTS", view.w / 2, view.h * .39 + 25); }
   391	  }
   392	
   393	  function frame(now) {
   394	    const dt = Math.min(.034, Math.max(.001, (now - lastFrame) / 1000)); lastFrame = now; update(dt); render(); requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   395	  }
   396	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   397	})();

    37	  const rng = (start) => {
    38	    let n = (start >>> 0) || 1;

    67	  function makePlayer() {
    68	    return { x: 64, y: 106, hp: 5, maxHp: 5, facing: -Math.PI / 2, hurt: 0, attackCd: 0, attackTime: 0, invuln: 0, walk: 0 };
    69	  }

    71	  function makeWorld() {
    72	    tiles = new Uint8Array(MAP * MAP);
    73	    explored = new Uint8Array(MAP * MAP);
    74	    for (let y = 0; y < MAP; y++) {
    75	      for (let x = 0; x < MAP; x++) {
    76	        const dx = (x - 63.5) / 1.02, dy = (y - 63.5) / .91;
    77	        const edge = Math.hypot(dx, dy) + (hash(x, y) - .5) * 7;
    78	        let tile = WATER;
    79	        if (edge < 60) {
    80	          const patch = (Math.sin(x * .15 + seed * .00001) + Math.cos(y * .12 - seed * .00002) + hash(x >> 1, y >> 1) * 1.8) / 3;
    81	          tile = edge > 53 ? BEACH : patch > .44 ? FOREST : patch < -.46 ? ROCK : GRASS;
    82	          if (hash(x + 70, y - 25) > .985) tile = RUIN;
    83	        } else if (edge < 64) tile = BEACH;
    84	        tiles[y * MAP + x] = tile;
    85	      }
    86	    }
    87	    // Keep the important landmarks on the island even with a rough seed.
    88	    [ [64, 106, 6], [64, 51, 5], [31, 39, 3], [99, 43, 3], [74, 94, 3] ].forEach(([cx, cy, r]) => {
    89	      for (let y = cy - r; y <= cy + r; y++) for (let x = cx - r; x <= cx + r; x++) {
    90	        if (x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < MAP && y < MAP && dist(x, y, cx, cy) <= r) tiles[y * MAP + x] = y > cy + r - 2 ? BEACH : GRASS;
    91	      }
    92	    });
    93	    for (let y = 48; y <= 54; y++) for (let x = 60; x <= 68; x++) if (dist(x, y, 64, 51) < 5) tiles[y * MAP + x] = RUIN;
    94	    const points = [[31,39],[99,43],[74,94]];
    95	    worldEnemies = [];
    96	    const types = ['mossling', 'skitter', 'brute'];
    97	    const r = rng(seed ^ 0xa91e37);
    98	    for (let i = 0; i < 17; i++) {
    99	      let x = 18 + r() * 92, y = 18 + r() * 92, tries = 0;
   100	      while ((!isLand(x, y) || dist(x, y, 64, 106) < 11 || points.some(p => dist(x, y, p[0], p[1]) < 7)) && tries++ < 20) { x = 18 + r() * 92; y = 18 + r() * 92; }
   101	      if (isLand(x, y)) worldEnemies.push({ type: types[i % 3], x, y, homeX: x, homeY: y, hp: 2 + (i % 3 === 2 ? 1 : 0), maxHp: 2 + (i % 3 === 2 ? 1 : 0), phase: r() * TAU, cooldown: 0, hit: 0, charge: 0, dead: false });
   102	    }
   103	    hearts = [];
   104	    particles = [];
   105	  }

   107	  function isLand(x, y) {
   108	    const tx = Math.floor(x), ty = Math.floor(y);
   109	    return tx >= 1 && ty >= 1 && tx < MAP - 1 && ty < MAP - 1 && tiles[ty * MAP + tx] !== WATER;
   110	  }
   111	  function reveal(x, y, radius = 7) {
   112	    const minX = Math.max(0, Math.floor(x - radius)), maxX = Math.min(MAP - 1, Math.ceil(x + radius));
   113	    const minY = Math.max(0, Math.floor(y - radius)), maxY = Math.min(MAP - 1, Math.ceil(y + radius));
   114	    for (let yy = minY; yy <= maxY; yy++) for (let xx = minX; xx <= maxX; xx++) if (dist(x, y, xx + .5, yy + .5) < radius) explored[yy * MAP + xx] = 1;
   115	  }

   123	  function loadGame() {
   124	    let saved = null;
   125	    try { saved = JSON.parse(store ? store.getItem(SAVE_KEY) || 'null' : 'null'); } catch (_) {}
   126	    seed = saved && saved.seed ? saved.seed >>> 0 : (Math.random() * 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
   127	    gear = saved && saved.gear ? { sword: saved.gear.sword || 0, armor: saved.gear.armor || 0, speed: saved.gear.speed || 0 } : { sword: 0, armor: 0, speed: 0 };
   128	    relics = saved && Array.isArray(saved.relics) ? saved.relics.slice(0, 3).map(Boolean) : [false, false, false];
   129	    makeWorld();
   130	    if (saved && saved.explored) {
   131	      const old = saved.explored;
   132	      for (let i = 0; i < explored.length && i < old.length; i++) explored[i] = old.charCodeAt(i) ? 1 : 0;
   133	    }
   134	    player = makePlayer();
   135	    applyGear();
   136	  }

   138	  function saveGame() {
   139	    try {

   143	      if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; if (store) store.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(bestScore)); }
   144	    } catch (_) {}
   145	  }

   180	  function moveEntity(entity, dx, dy, dt, world = false) {
   181	    const nx = entity.x + dx * dt, ny = entity.y + dy * dt;
   182	    if (!world || isLand(nx, entity.y)) entity.x = nx;
   183	    if (!world || isLand(entity.x, ny)) entity.y = ny;
   184	  }

   186	  function movementVector() {
   187	    let x = input.x, y = input.y;
   188	    if (Math.abs(x) + Math.abs(y) < .05) {
   189	      x = (keys.d || keys.arrowright ? 1 : 0) - (keys.a || keys.arrowleft ? 1 : 0);
   190	      y = (keys.s || keys.arrowdown ? 1 : 0) - (keys.w || keys.arrowup ? 1 : 0);
   191	    }
   192	    const len = Math.hypot(x, y);
   193	    return len > 1 ? { x: x / len, y: y / len } : { x, y };
   194	  }

   196	  function updatePlayer(dt, world = true) {
   197	    const mv = movementVector();
   198	    const speed = 5.3 + gear.speed * .75;
   199	    if (mv.x || mv.y) {
   200	      player.facing = Math.atan2(mv.y, mv.x); player.walk += dt * 10;
   201	      moveEntity(player, mv.x * speed, mv.y * speed, dt, world);
   202	    }
   203	    player.attackCd = Math.max(0, player.attackCd - dt);
   204	    player.attackTime = Math.max(0, player.attackTime - dt);
   205	    player.invuln = Math.max(0, player.invuln - dt);
   206	    player.hurt = Math.max(0, player.hurt - dt);
   207	    if (input.action || keys.space || keys.enter) tryAction();
   208	  }
   209	
   210	  function tryAction() {
   211	    if (runState !== 'play') return;
   212	    if (!input.actionPressed && !(input.action || keys.space || keys.enter)) return;
   213	    // Held action repeats attacks, but interaction consumes the press once.
   214	    const press = input.actionPressed;
   215	    if (mode === 'world') {
   216	      const p = player;
   217	      const entrances = [[31,39],[99,43],[74,94]];
   218	      for (let i = 0; i < entrances.length; i++) if (!relics[i] && dist(p.x, p.y, entrances[i][0], entrances[i][1]) < 2.3) { enterDungeon(i); input.actionPressed = false; return; }
   219	      if (dist(p.x, p.y, 64, 51) < 3.2) {
   220	        if (relics.every(Boolean)) enterBoss(); else setMessage(`A sealed ruin • ${relics.filter(Boolean).length}/3 relics`, 2);
   221	        input.actionPressed = false; return;
   222	      }
   223	      if (press || input.action) attackPlayer();
   224	    } else if (mode === 'dungeon') {
   225	      if (dungeon.cleared && dungeon.room === 4 && dist(player.x, player.y, 10, 6) < 2.5) { openChest(); input.actionPressed = false; return; }
   226	      if (dungeon.cleared && dungeon.room < 4 && player.y < 2.3) { nextRoom(); input.actionPressed = false; return; }
   227	      if (press || input.action) attackPlayer();
   228	    } else if (mode === 'boss') {
   229	      if (press || input.action) attackPlayer();
   230	    }
   231	    input.actionPressed = false;
   232	  }
   233	
   234	  function attackPlayer() {
   235	    if (player.attackCd > 0 || runState !== 'play') return;
   236	    player.attackCd = .34; player.attackTime = .23; shake = Math.max(shake, .08);
   237	    const damage = 1 + gear.sword;
   238	    const targets = mode === 'world' ? worldEnemies : mode === 'dungeon' ? dungeon.enemies : [boss];
   239	    for (const e of targets) {
   240	      if (!e || e.dead || e.hp <= 0) continue;
   241	      const range = mode === 'boss' ? 2.25 : mode === 'dungeon' ? 2.0 : 1.85;
   242	      const dd = dist(player.x, player.y, e.x, e.y);
   243	      const bearing = Math.atan2(e.y - player.y, e.x - player.x);
   244	      const angleDiff = Math.atan2(Math.sin(bearing - player.facing), Math.cos(bearing - player.facing));
   245	      if (dd < range && Math.abs(angleDiff) < 1.25) {
   246	        e.hp -= damage; e.hit = .22;
   247	        const push = mode === 'boss' ? .6 : .9;
   248	        e.x += Math.cos(bearing) * push; e.y += Math.sin(bearing) * push;
   249	        burst(e.x, e.y, '#f6d88c', 7); shake = Math.max(shake, .14);
   250	        if (e.hp <= 0) killEnemy(e);
   251	      }
   252	    }
   253	  }

   260	    if (mode === 'boss') win();

   263	  function hurt(amount, fromX, fromY) {
   264	    if (player.invuln > 0 || runState !== 'play') return;
   265	    player.hp -= amount; player.invuln = .8; player.hurt = .25; shake = .32; flash = .18;
   266	    const a = Math.atan2(player.y - fromY, player.x - fromX);
   267	    if (mode === 'world') moveEntity(player, Math.cos(a) * 1.1, Math.sin(a) * 1.1, 1, true);
   268	    burst(player.x, player.y, '#ff7968', 8);
   269	    if (player.hp <= 0) die();
   270	  }

   293	  function buildDungeonEnemies(index, room) {
   294	    const r = rng(seed + index * 991 + room * 173);
   295	    const list = [], count = 2 + room + (index % 2);
   296	    const types = index === 0 ? ['skitter','mossling'] : index === 1 ? ['brute','wisp'] : ['wisp','skitter','brute'];
   297	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   298	      const type = types[i % types.length];
   299	      list.push({ type, x: 3 + r() * 14, y: 2.5 + r() * 7, hp: type === 'brute' ? 4 : type === 'wisp' ? 2 : 3, maxHp: type === 'brute' ? 4 : type === 'wisp' ? 2 : 3, phase: r() * TAU, cooldown: .4 + r(), hit: 0, dead: false, charge: 0 });
   300	    }
   301	    return list;
   302	  }

   325	  function updateDungeon(dt) {
   326	    updatePlayer(dt, false);
   327	    for (const e of dungeon.enemies) {
   328	      if (e.dead) continue;
   329	      e.cooldown = Math.max(0, e.cooldown - dt); e.hit = Math.max(0, e.hit - dt); e.phase += dt;
   330	      const dx = player.x - e.x, dy = player.y - e.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   331	      let vx = Math.cos(e.phase) * .25, vy = Math.sin(e.phase * .8) * .25;
   332	      if (e.type === 'mossling') { vx = dx / d; vy = dy / d; }
   333	      if (e.type === 'skitter') { const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.sin(e.phase * 2.4) * .85; vx = Math.cos(a); vy = Math.sin(a); }
   334	      if (e.type === 'brute') { if (d < 9) { vx = dx / d; vy = dy / d; } }
   335	      if (e.type === 'wisp') { const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.sin(e.phase) * 1.1; vx = Math.cos(a); vy = Math.sin(a); }
   336	      const speed = e.type === 'brute' ? .75 : e.type === 'wisp' ? 1.15 : 1.45;
   337	      moveEntity(e, vx * speed, vy * speed, dt, false); e.x = clamp(e.x, 1.3, 18.7); e.y = clamp(e.y, 1.5, 10.5);
   338	      if (d < (e.type === 'brute' ? 1.1 : .75) && e.cooldown <= 0) { e.cooldown = e.type === 'brute' ? 1.3 : .85; hurt(e.type === 'brute' ? 2 : 1, e.x, e.y); }
   339	    }
   340	  }

   342	  function updateBoss(dt) {
   343	    updatePlayer(dt, false);
   344	    if (!boss || boss.dead) return;
   345	    boss.phase += dt; boss.cooldown -= dt; boss.hit = Math.max(0, boss.hit - dt); boss.pulse = Math.max(0, boss.pulse - dt);
   346	    const dx = player.x - boss.x, dy = player.y - boss.y, d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
   347	    const a = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.sin(boss.phase * 1.4) * .35;
   348	    moveEntity(boss, Math.cos(a) * 1.15, Math.sin(a) * 1.15, dt, false); boss.x = clamp(boss.x, 2, 18); boss.y = clamp(boss.y, 2, 9.5);
   349	    if (boss.cooldown <= 0) { boss.cooldown = 2.0; boss.pulse = .45; burst(boss.x, boss.y, '#e76a62', 10); }
   350	    if (boss.pulse > 0 && d < 3.3) hurt(1, boss.x, boss.y);
   351	    if (d < 1.25 && boss.cooldown < 1.1 && boss.cooldown > .8) hurt(2, boss.x, boss.y);
   352	  }
   353	
   354	  function burst(x, y, color, count) {
   355	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) { const a = Math.random() * TAU, s = .8 + Math.random() * 3.2; particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s, life: .25 + Math.random() * .5, max: .75, color, size: 1.5 + Math.random() * 3 }); }
   356	  }
   357	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   358	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const p = particles[i]; p.life -= dt; p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.vx *= .97; p.vy *= .97; if (p.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1); }
   359	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 1.8); flash = Math.max(0, flash - dt * 2.4);
   360	  }
   361	
   362	  function update(dt) {
   363	    if (runState !== 'play' && input.actionPressed) {
   364	      input.actionPressed = false;
   365	      if (runState === 'dead') respawn(); else newAdventure();
   366	    }
   367	    if (runState === 'play') {
   368	      elapsed += dt;
   369	      if (mode === 'world') updateWorld(dt);
   370	      else if (mode === 'dungeon') updateDungeon(dt);
   371	      else updateBoss(dt);
   372	      if (messageTime > 0) { messageTime -= dt; if (messageTime <= 0) setMessage(mode === 'world' ? 'Find the hidden gates. ACTION attacks / opens.' : 'Move with the stick. ACTION slashes.', 3); }
   373	      saveClock += dt;
   374	      if (saveClock >= 8) { saveClock = 0; saveGame(); }
   375	    }
   376	    updateParticles(dt);
   377	    render(); updateHud();
   378	  }

   382	    const relicText = relics ? relics.map(v => v ? '◆' : '◇').join('') : '◇◇◇';
   383	    const gearText = gear ? `S${gear.sword} A${gear.armor} V${gear.speed}` : '';

   385	    if (messageTime > 0) hintEl.textContent = message;
   386	  }

   393	  function tileColor(t) { return t === WATER ? '#123846' : t === BEACH ? '#c5a86c' : t === FOREST ? '#285942' : t === ROCK ? '#596268' : t === RUIN ? '#6c5963' : '#477d55'; }

   397	    ctx.save();

   463	    const r = stickEl.getBoundingClientRect(), cx = r.left + r.width/2, cy = r.top + r.height/2, max = 35;

    28	function mulberry32(a) {
    29	  return function () {
    30	    a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    31	    var t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
    32	    t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    33	    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    34	  };
    35	}

   109	function genWorld(sd) {
   110	  var rng = mulberry32(sd);
   111	  var s1 = rng() * 100, s2 = rng() * 100, s3 = rng() * 100;
   112	  rows = new Array(NROWS + 1);
   113	  var i;
   114	  for (i = 0; i <= NROWS; i++) {
   115	    var d = i * ROW, L, R;
   116	    if (d < 120) { L = 30; R = WW - 30; }
   117	    else {
   118	      var t = d - 120;
   119	      var base = 168 - Math.min(96, t * 0.036);
   120	      var wob = Math.sin(t * 0.0062 + s1) * 78 + Math.sin(t * 0.0171 + s2) * 34 + Math.sin(t * 0.041 + s3) * 14;
   121	      var hw = base + Math.sin(t * 0.029 + s3 * 2) * 26 + (rng() - 0.5) * 26;
   122	      hw = clamp(hw, 58, 200);
   123	      var cx = clamp(WW / 2 + wob, hw + 26, WW - hw - 26);
   124	      L = cx - hw; R = cx + hw;
   125	      if (d < 260) { var b = (d - 120) / 140; L = lerp(30, L, b); R = lerp(WW - 30, R, b); }
   126	    }
   127	    rows[i] = { l: L, r: R, ll: -9, rl: -9 };
   128	  }
   129	  // smoothing pass
   130	  for (var p = 0; p < 2; p++) {
   131	    for (i = 1; i < NROWS; i++) {
   132	      rows[i].l = (rows[i - 1].l + rows[i].l * 2 + rows[i + 1].l) / 4;
   133	      rows[i].r = (rows[i - 1].r + rows[i].r * 2 + rows[i + 1].r) / 4;
   134	    }
   135	  }
   136	  // crates
   137	  crates = [];
   138	  var slots = [], nCr = 7;
   139	  for (i = 0; i < nCr; i++) slots.push(6 + Math.floor((i / nCr) * (NROWS - 12) + rng() * 5));
   140	  for (i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) {
   141	    var ri = clamp(slots[i], 5, NROWS - 2), rr = rows[ri];
   142	    crates.push({
   143	      x: lerp(rr.l + 26, rr.r - 26, 0.18 + rng() * 0.64),
   144	      y: ri * ROW + rng() * ROW, lit: -9, got: false, sp: rng() * 6
   145	    });
   146	  }
   147	  // fauna: big listeners + small drifters
   148	  fauna = [];

   153	      x: lerp(f0.l + 30, f0.r - 30, rng()), y: fr * ROW,

   169	function wallAt(y) {
   170	  var fi = clamp(y / ROW, 0, NROWS - 0.001);
   171	  var i = Math.floor(fi), t = fi - i;
   172	  var a = rows[i], b = rows[Math.min(NROWS, i + 1)];
   173	  return { l: lerp(a.l, b.l, t), r: lerp(a.r, b.r, t) };

   202	function firePing() {

   214	function pointerDown(x, y, id) {
   215	  audioInit();

   224	  if (inBtn(btnP, x, y)) { firePing(); return; }
   225	  if (!touchSteer.active) { touchSteer.active = true; touchSteer.id = id; touchSteer.x0 = x; touchSteer.dx = 0; }
   226	}
   227	function pointerMove(x, y, id) {
   228	  if (touchSteer.active && touchSteer.id === id) touchSteer.dx = clamp(x - touchSteer.x0, -70, 70);
   229	}
   230	function pointerUp(id) {
   231	  if (touchSteer.active && touchSteer.id === id) { touchSteer.active = false; touchSteer.dx = 0; touchSteer.id = -1; }
   232	  if (ballastTouch === id) { held = false; ballastTouch = -1; }
   233	}

   433	function endDive(win, why) {
   434	  state = S.OVER; overT = 0; overWin = win;
   435	  meta.dives++;
   436	  if (win) {
   437	    meta.bank += sub.carry;
   438	    overText = sub.carry >= 3 ? 'DIVE COMPLETE' : 'PARTIAL HAUL';
   439	    sfxWin();

   442	    sfxDead();
   443	    burst(sub.x, sub.y, 40, '255,120,80', 200);
   444	    shake = 20;
   445	  }
   446	  save();
   447	}

   558	        ctx.lineTo(f.x - f.r * 2.3 - Math.sin(f.ph + t2) * 8, f.y + oy + Math.cos(f.ph + t2) * 7);
   559	      }
   560	      ctx.stroke();
   561	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,220,120,' + (fa * 0.9).toFixed(3) + ')';
   562	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(f.x + f.r * 0.7, f.y - f.r * 0.25, 2.6, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   563	    }
   564	  }
   565	
   566	  // ping rings
   567	  for (var pi = 0; pi < pings.length; pi++) {
   568	    var p = pings[pi], pa = 1 - p.r / p.max;
   569	    ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,240,255,' + (pa * 0.55).toFixed(3) + ')';
   570	    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
   571	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, p.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   572	  }
   573	
   574	  // bubbles
   575	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(170,230,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   576	  for (var bi = 0; bi < bubbles.length; bi++) {
   577	    var bb = bubbles[bi];
   578	    ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, 1 - bb.t / 2.2) * 0.6;
   579	    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(bb.x, bb.y, bb.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   580	  }
   581	  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   582	
   583	  // particles
   584	  for (var pj = 0; pj < parts.length; pj++) {
   585	    var pt = parts[pj], al2 = Math.max(0, 1 - pt.t / pt.life);
   586	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(' + pt.c + ',' + al2.toFixed(3) + ')';
   587	    ctx.fillRect(pt.x - 1.6, pt.y - 1.6, 3.2, 3.2);
   588	  }
   589	
   590	  // sub
   591	  if (state === S.PLAY || overWin) drawSub();
   592	
   593	  ctx.restore();
   594	
   595	  drawHUD();
   596	}
   597	
   598	function drawSub() {
   599	  var glow = ctx.createRadialGradient(sub.x, sub.y, 4, sub.x, sub.y, 130);
   600	  var flash = now - sub.dmgT < 0.22;
   601	  glow.addColorStop(0, flash ? 'rgba(255,120,110,0.42)' : 'rgba(120,220,255,0.24)');
   602	  glow.addColorStop(0.45, 'rgba(80,180,240,0.07)');
   603	  glow.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
   604	  ctx.fillStyle = glow;
   605	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sub.x, sub.y, 130, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   606	
   607	  ctx.save();
   608	  ctx.translate(sub.x, sub.y); ctx.rotate(sub.tilt * 0.5);
   609	  ctx.strokeStyle = flash ? '#ffb0a8' : '#bff0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
   610	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(20,52,74,0.9)';
   611	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 0, 17, 9.5, 0, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
   612	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-2, -9.5); ctx.lineTo(-2, -16); ctx.lineTo(7, -16); ctx.lineTo(7, -9.5); ctx.stroke();
   613	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-16, 0); ctx.lineTo(-23, -6); ctx.moveTo(-16, 0); ctx.lineTo(-23, 6); ctx.stroke();
   614	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe27a';
   615	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(9, -1, 2.6, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill();
   616	  ctx.restore();
   617	
   618	  // carry markers
   619	  for (var i = 0; i < sub.carry; i++) {
   620	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffc45a';
   621	    ctx.fillRect(sub.x - 12 + i * 9, sub.y + 15, 6, 6);
   622	  }
   623	}

   625	function bar(x, y, w, h, v, col, bg) {
   626	  ctx.fillStyle = bg || 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)';
   627	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
   628	  ctx.fillStyle = col;
   629	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w * clamp(v, 0, 1), h);
   630	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.22)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   631	  ctx.strokeRect(x + 0.5, y + 0.5, w - 1, h - 1);
   632	}

   634	function drawHUD() {
   635	  layoutButtons();
   636	  ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
   637	
   638	  if (state === S.PLAY || state === S.OVER) {
   639	    // top bars
   640	    var pad = 12, bw = Math.min(150, (W - pad * 3) / 2);
   641	    ctx.font = '10px ui-monospace,monospace';
   642	    ctx.textAlign = 'left';
   643	    ctx.fillStyle = '#7fd4ef';
   644	    ctx.fillText('AIR', pad, 16);
   645	    bar(pad, 23, bw, 9, air / airMax(), air < 26 ? (Math.floor(now * 6) % 2 ? '#ff6a6a' : '#ffb36a') : '#6fe0ff');
   646	    ctx.textAlign = 'right';
   647	    ctx.fillStyle = '#7fd4ef';
   648	    ctx.fillText('HULL', W - pad, 16);
   649	    bar(W - pad - bw, 23, bw, 9, hull / hullMax(), hull < hullMax() * 0.3 ? '#ff6a6a' : '#9dffc4');
   650	
   651	    // depth
   652	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   653	    var d = Math.floor(sub.y);
   654	    var rl = redline();
   655	    ctx.font = 'bold 22px ui-monospace,monospace';
   656	    ctx.fillStyle = d > rl ? (Math.floor(now * 5) % 2 ? '#ff6a6a' : '#ffd0a0') : '#c9f4ff';
   657	    ctx.fillText(d + ' m', W / 2, 26);
   658	    ctx.font = '9px ui-monospace,monospace';
   659	    ctx.fillStyle = d > rl ? '#ff8a8a' : 'rgba(150,220,245,0.6)';
   660	    ctx.fillText(d > rl ? 'PRESSURE CRITICAL' : 'REDLINE ' + rl + ' m', W / 2, 43);
   661	
   662	    // salvage
   663	    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   664	    ctx.font = '11px ui-monospace,monospace';
   665	    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffc45a';
   666	    ctx.fillText('SALVAGE ' + sub.carry + '/3   BANK ' + meta.bank, W / 2, 60);
   667	
   668	    // depth tape (right edge)
   669	    var tapeH = H - 200, ty = 78;
   670	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)';
   671	    ctx.fillRect(W - 6, ty, 3, tapeH);
   672	    var rlY = ty + tapeH * (rl / MAXD);
   673	    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,90,90,0.7)'; ctx.fillRect(W - 9, rlY, 9, 2);
   674	    ctx.fillStyle = '#6fe0ff';
   675	    ctx.fillRect(W - 10, ty + tapeH * clamp(sub.y / MAXD, 0, 1) - 1, 11, 3);
   676	
   677	    // controls
   678	    var down = held || keys[' '] || keys['arrowdown'] || keys['s'];
   679	    drawRoundBtn(btnB, down ? 'rgba(110,230,255,0.30)' : 'rgba(110,230,255,0.12)', '#8fe3ff', 'BALLAST', down ? 'FLOOD' : 'HOLD');
   680	    var ready = pingCd <= 0;
   681	    drawRoundBtn(btnP, ready ? 'rgba(255,200,110,0.16)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)', ready ? '#ffd47a' : 'rgba(255,212,122,0.35)', 'SONAR', ready ? 'PING' : Math.max(0, pingCd).toFixed(1));
   682	    if (!ready) {
   683	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,212,122,0.7)'; ctx.lineWidth = 3;
   684	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(btnP.x, btnP.y, btnP.r, -1.5708, -1.5708 + 6.283 * (1 - pingCd / 1.5)); ctx.stroke();
   685	    }
   686	
   687	    // steer indicator
   688	    if (touchSteer.active) {
   689	      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(150,230,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   690	      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(touchSteer.x0 + touchSteer.dx, H * 0.5, 22, 0, 6.283); ctx.stroke();
   691	    }
   692

   698	      ctx.fillText('HOLD BALLAST to sink, DRAG to steer, PING to see - grab 3 crates and surface', W / 2, H * 0.62);
   699	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   700	    }

   705	      ctx.fillText(msgText, W / 2, H * 0.3);
   706	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   707	    }
   708	  }
   709	
   710	  uiBtns = [];
   711	  if (state === S.OVER) drawOver();
   712	  else if (state === S.SHOP) drawShop();
   713	}

   715	function drawRoundBtn(b, fill, stroke, t1, t2) {
   716	  ctx.fillStyle = fill; ctx.strokeStyle = stroke; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   717	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, 6.283); ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();

   722	  ctx.font = '10px ui-monospace,monospace';
   723	  ctx.fillText(t2, b.x, b.y + 9);
   724	}
