   726	function panel(y, h) {
   727	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(4,12,20,0.88)';
   728	  ctx.fillRect(0, y, W, h);
   729	  ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(110,220,255,0.35)'; ctx.lineWidth = 1;
   730	  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, y + 0.5); ctx.lineTo(W, y + 0.5);
   731	  ctx.moveTo(0, y + h - 0.5); ctx.lineTo(W, y + h - 0.5); ctx.stroke();
   732	}
   733	function uiButton(x, y, w, h, label, sub2, fn, enabled) {
   734	  ctx.fillStyle = enabled === false ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.05)' : 'rgba(110,230,255,0.15)';
   735	  ctx.fillRect(x, y, w, h);
   736	  ctx.strokeStyle = enabled === false ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)' : '#7fe0ff'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;
   737	  ctx.strokeRect(x + 1, y + 1, w - 2, h - 2);
   738	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   739	  ctx.fillStyle = enabled === false ? 'rgba(200,230,245,0.35)' : '#d6f6ff';
   740	  ctx.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace,monospace';
   741	  ctx.fillText(label, x + w / 2, y + (sub2 ? h / 2 - 8 : h / 2));
   742	  if (sub2) {
   743	    ctx.font = '10px ui-monospace,monospace';
   744	    ctx.fillStyle = enabled === false ? 'rgba(200,230,245,0.3)' : 'rgba(190,235,250,0.8)';
   745	    ctx.fillText(sub2, x + w / 2, y + h / 2 + 9);
   746	  }
   747	  if (enabled !== false) uiBtns.push({ x: x, y: y, w: w, h: h, fn: fn });
   748	}
   749	
   750	function drawOver() {
   751	  var ph = 230, py = H / 2 - ph / 2;
   752	  panel(py, ph);
   753	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   754	  ctx.font = 'bold 24px ui-monospace,monospace';
   755	  ctx.fillStyle = overWin ? '#9dffc4' : '#ff8a8a';
   756	  ctx.fillText(overText, W / 2, py + 38);
   757	  ctx.font = '12px ui-monospace,monospace';
   758	  ctx.fillStyle = '#bfeaff';
   759	  ctx.fillText(overWin ? 'Banked ' + sub.carry + ' salvage' : 'Salvage lost at depth', W / 2, py + 66);
   760	  ctx.fillText('Depth reached ' + Math.floor(sub.y) + ' m   Best ' + meta.best + ' m', W / 2, py + 88);
   761	  ctx.font = 'bold 14px ui-monospace,monospace';
   762	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffc45a';
   763	  ctx.fillText('BANK: ' + meta.bank + ' SALVAGE', W / 2, py + 114);
   764	  var bw = Math.min(240, W - 60);
   765	  uiButton(W / 2 - bw / 2, py + 132, bw, 44, 'DIVE AGAIN', null, function () { startDive(); });
   766	  uiButton(W / 2 - bw / 2, py + 180, bw, 40, 'DRY DOCK', 'upgrade air & hull', function () { state = S.SHOP; });
   767	}
   768	
   769	function drawShop() {
   770	  var ph = 300, py = H / 2 - ph / 2;
   771	  panel(py, ph);
   772	  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
   773	  ctx.font = 'bold 20px ui-monospace,monospace';
   774	  ctx.fillStyle = '#bfeaff';
   775	  ctx.fillText('DRY DOCK', W / 2, py + 30);
   776	  ctx.font = 'bold 13px ui-monospace,monospace';
   777	  ctx.fillStyle = '#ffc45a';
   778	  ctx.fillText('BANK: ' + meta.bank + ' SALVAGE', W / 2, py + 54);
   779	  var bw = Math.min(260, W - 50), bx = W / 2 - bw / 2;
   780	  var ca = upAirCost(), ch = upHullCost();
   781	  uiButton(bx, py + 72, bw, 52,
   782	    'AIR TANKS Lv' + (meta.air + 1),
   783	    Math.round(airMax()) + 's -> ' + Math.round(airMax() + 28) + 's   cost ' + ca,
   784	    function () { if (meta.bank >= ca) { meta.bank -= ca; meta.air++; save(); sfxPickup(); } },
   785	    meta.bank >= ca);
   786	  uiButton(bx, py + 132, bw, 52,
   787	    'HULL PLATING Lv' + (meta.hull + 1),
   788	    'redline ' + redline() + 'm -> ' + (redline() + 340) + 'm   cost ' + ch,
   789	    function () { if (meta.bank >= ch) { meta.bank -= ch; meta.hull++; save(); sfxPickup(); } },
   790	    meta.bank >= ch);
   791	  ctx.font = '10px ui-monospace,monospace';
   792	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(180,225,245,0.65)';
   793	  ctx.fillText('best depth ' + meta.best + ' m   dives ' + meta.dives, W / 2, py + 202);
   794	  uiButton(bx, py + 218, bw, 48, 'LAUNCH DIVE', null, function () { startDive(); });
   795	  ctx.font = '9px ui-monospace,monospace';
   796	  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(180,225,245,0.5)';
   797	  ctx.fillText('salvage banks only when you surface alive', W / 2, py + 282);
   798	}
   799	
   800	// ---------- loop ----------
   801	var last = 0;
   802	function frame(t) {
   803	  var dt = last ? (t - last) / 1000 : 0.016;
   804	  last = t;
   805	  if (dt > 0.05) dt = 0.05;
   806	  update(dt);
   807	  render();
   808	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   809	}
   810	startDive();
   811	requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   812	})();

    63	  function rngFactory(seed) {
    64	    let s = seed >>> 0;
    65	    return function () {
    66	      s += 0x6D2B79F5;
    67	      let t = s;
    68	      t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
    69	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
    70	      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    71	    };
    72	  }

   192	  function harborPrice(harbor, index) {
   193	    const drift = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.11 + harbor.marketSeed + index * 1.8) * 0.14;
   194	    const tide = Math.sin(elapsed * 0.037 + harbor.marketSeed * 2.1 + index) * 0.09;
   195	    return Math.max(8, Math.round(GOODS[index].base * clamp(harbor.bias[index] + drift + tide, 0.52, 1.65)));
   196	  }

   200	  function nearbyHarbor() {

   202	    let best = 100000;

   205	      const d = distance(player, dock);
   206	      if (d < 105 && d < best) { best = d; found = i; }
   207	    });
   208	    return found;
   209	  }

   294	  function getSailData() {
   295	    const windFrom = wrapAngle(wind.angle + Math.PI - player.heading);
   296	    const point = Math.abs(windFrom);
   297	    const noGo = 0.46;
   298	    const broadReach = 0.18 + 0.74 * Math.sin(clamp(point, 0, Math.PI)) + 0.18 * (1 - Math.cos(point)) / 2;
   299	    const idealTrim = clamp(windFrom * 0.74, -1.42, 1.42);
   300	    const trimError = Math.abs(wrapAngle(player.sailTrim - idealTrim));
   301	    const trimEfficiency = clamp(1 - trimError / 1.42, 0, 1);
   302	    const pointEfficiency = point < noGo ? 0.06 + point / noGo * 0.08 : broadReach;
   303	    const speed = (57 + player.hullLevel * 10) * pointEfficiency * (0.38 + trimEfficiency * 0.62);
   304	    return { windFrom, point, noGo, idealTrim, trimEfficiency, speed };
   305	  }
   306	
   307	  function updateWind(dt) {
   308	    elapsed += dt;
   309	    wind.angle = -0.68 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.022) * 0.48 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.0061) * 0.24;
   310	    wind.speed = 72 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.03) * 12 + Math.sin(elapsed * 0.008) * 6;
   311	  }
   312	
   313	  function updateStorms(dt) {
   314	    storms.forEach((storm, i) => {
   315	      storm.x += Math.cos(wind.angle) * (4 + i * 0.5) * dt;
   316	      storm.y += Math.sin(wind.angle) * (4 + i * 0.5) * dt;
   317	      if (storm.x < -storm.size) storm.x = WORLD + storm.size;
   318	      if (storm.x > WORLD + storm.size) storm.x = -storm.size;
   319	      if (storm.y < -storm.size) storm.y = WORLD + storm.size;
   320	      if (storm.y > WORLD + storm.size) storm.y = -storm.size;
   321	      storm.hitClock -= dt;
   322	    });
   323	  }
   324	
   325	  function update(dt) {
   326	    updateWind(dt);
   327	    updateStorms(dt);
   328	    if (marketOpen) {
   329	      updateParticles(dt);
   330	      saveClock += dt;
   331	      if (saveClock > 8) { saveClock = 0; saveGame(); }
   332	      return;
   333	    }
   334	
   335	    const steer = (keys.ArrowRight || keys.d || keys.D ? 1 : 0) - (keys.ArrowLeft || keys.a || keys.A ? 1 : 0);
   336	    const trim = (keys.ArrowUp || keys.w || keys.W ? 1 : 0) - (keys.ArrowDown || keys.s || keys.S ? 1 : 0);
   337	    if (steer) player.targetHeading = wrapAngle(player.targetHeading + steer * 1.2 * dt);
   338	    if (trim) player.sailTrim = clamp(player.sailTrim + trim * 1.05 * dt, -1.45, 1.45);
   339	    player.heading = lerpAngle(player.heading, player.targetHeading, 1 - Math.exp(-2.5 * dt));
   340	    player.sailTrim = clamp(player.sailTrim, -1.45, 1.45);
   341	
   342	    const sailing = getSailData();
   343	    let inStorm = false;
   344	    let stormHit = null;
   345	    for (const storm of storms) {
   346	      if (Math.abs(player.x - storm.x) < storm.size * 0.52 && Math.abs(player.y - storm.y) < storm.size * 0.52) {
   347	        inStorm = true;
   348	        stormHit = storm;
   349	        break;
   350	      }
   351	    }
   352	    player.inStorm = inStorm;
   353	    if (inStorm && stormHit) {
   354	      player.stormClock += dt;
   355	      if (stormHit.hitClock <= 0) {
   356	        stormHit.hitClock = 4.5;
   357	        if (cargoCount() > 0) {
   358	          const loaded = player.cargo.findIndex((n) => n > 0);
   359	          if (loaded >= 0) {
   360	            player.cargo[loaded]--;
   361	            setMessage('Storm took 1 ' + GOODS[loaded].name, 2.2);
   362	            screenShake = 0.2;
   363	            addBurst(player.x, player.y, '#7e8ca9', 12, 0.5);
   364	          }
   365	        }
   366	      }
   367	    } else player.stormClock = 0;
   368	
   369	    const stormBoost = inStorm ? 1.28 : 1;
   370	    const targetSpeed = sailing.speed * stormBoost;
   371	    player.speed = lerp(player.speed, targetSpeed, 1 - Math.exp(-3.5 * dt));
   372	    const before = { x: player.x, y: player.y };
   373	    player.x += Math.cos(player.heading) * player.speed * dt;
   374	    player.y += Math.sin(player.heading) * player.speed * dt;
   375	    player.x = clamp(player.x, 46, WORLD - 46);
   376	    player.y = clamp(player.y, 46, WORLD - 46);
   377	
   378	    islands.forEach((island) => {
   379	      const dx = player.x - island.x;
   380	      const dy = player.y - island.y;
   381	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
   382	      const minD = island.r + 14;
   383	      if (d < minD) {
   384	        const nx = d ? dx / d : 1;
   385	        const ny = d ? dy / d : 0;
   386	        player.x = island.x + nx * minD;
   387	        player.y = island.y + ny * minD;
   388	        player.speed *= 0.25;
   389	        player.targetHeading = wrapAngle(Math.atan2(ny, nx) + 0.9);
   390	        screenShake = Math.max(screenShake, 0.07);
   391	      }
   392	    });
   393	
   394	    player.wakeClock += dt;
   395	    if (player.wakeClock > 0.06 && distance(before, player) > 0.6) {
   396	      player.wakeClock = 0;
   397	      const p = { x: player.x - Math.cos(player.heading) * 17, y: player.y - Math.sin(player.heading) * 17 };
   398	      particles.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: 0, vy: 0, life: 0.8, max: 0.8, color: '#caece7', size: 2 + Math.random() * 2 });
   399	    }
   400	    updateParticles(dt);
   401	    camera.x = lerp(camera.x, player.x, 1 - Math.exp(-4 * dt));
   402	    camera.y = lerp(camera.y, player.y, 1 - Math.exp(-4 * dt));
   403	    screenShake = Math.max(0, screenShake - dt * 0.7);
   404	    saveClock += dt;
   405	    if (saveClock > 6) { saveClock = 0; saveGame(); }
   406	    if (messageClock > 0) messageClock -= dt;
   407	    checkWin();
   408	  }

   410	  function addBurst(wx, wy, color, count, force) {
   411	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
   412	      const a = Math.random() * TAU;
   413	      const speed = (12 + Math.random() * 34) * (force || 1);
   414	      particles.push({ x: wx, y: wy, vx: Math.cos(a) * speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * speed, life: 0.45 + Math.random() * 0.65, max: 1.1, color, size: 2 + Math.random() * 3 });
   415	    }
   416	  }
   417	
   418	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   419	    particles = particles.filter((p) => {
   420	      p.life -= dt;
   421	      p.x += p.vx * dt;
   422	      p.y += p.vy * dt;
   423	      p.vx *= Math.pow(0.04, dt);
   424	      p.vy *= Math.pow(0.04, dt);
   425	      return p.life > 0;
   426	    });
   427	  }

   737	  function pointerPos(event) {

   742	  function isInside(x, y, rx, ry, rw, rh) { return x >= rx && x <= rx + rw && y >= ry && y <= ry + rh; }

   744	  function handlePointerDown(event) {
   745	    event.preventDefault();
   746	    const p = pointerPos(event);
   747	    pointer = { x: p.x, y: p.y, down: true, moved: false, draggingTrim: false, id: event.pointerId };
   748	    if (canvas.setPointerCapture) canvas.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);
   749	    if (!marketOpen && !won && Math.hypot(p.x - (W - 62), p.y - (H - 86)) < 58) {
   750	      pointer.draggingTrim = true;
   751	      updateTrimFromPointer(p.x, p.y);
   752	    }
   753	  }
   754	
   755	  function handlePointerMove(event) {
   756	    if (!pointer.down || (pointer.id !== null && event.pointerId !== pointer.id)) return;
   757	    event.preventDefault();
   758	    const p = pointerPos(event);
   759	    if (Math.hypot(p.x - pointer.x, p.y - pointer.y) > 5) pointer.moved = true;
   760	    pointer.x = p.x; pointer.y = p.y;
   761	    if (pointer.draggingTrim) updateTrimFromPointer(p.x, p.y);
   762	  }
   763	
   764	  function updateTrimFromPointer(x, y) {
   765	    const dx = x - (W - 62); const dy = y - (H - 86);
   766	    if (Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 10) return;
   767	    const dialAngle = Math.atan2(dy, dx) + Math.PI / 2;
   768	    player.sailTrim = clamp(wrapAngle(dialAngle), -1.45, 1.45);
   769	  }
   770	
   771	  function handlePointerUp(event) {
   772	    if (pointer.id !== null && event.pointerId !== pointer.id) return;
   773	    event.preventDefault();
   774	    const p = pointerPos(event);
   775	    const wasTap = pointer.down && !pointer.moved && !pointer.draggingTrim;
   776	    const wasTrim = pointer.draggingTrim;
   777	    pointer.down = false; pointer.draggingTrim = false; pointer.id = null;
   778	    if (wasTrim) { setMessage('Trim set — aim across the wind for speed', 2); return; }
   779	    if (!wasTap) return;
   780	    if (won) {
   781	      if (isInside(p.x, p.y, W / 2 - 135, H * .25 + 290 - 57, 270, 54)) resetGame();
   782	      return;
   783	    }
   784	    if (marketOpen) { handleMarketTap(p.x, p.y); return; }
   785	    if (isInside(p.x, p.y, W - 58, 86, 52, 52)) { resetGame(); return; }
   786	    const world = worldPoint(p.x, p.y);
   787	    let tappedHarbor = -1;
   788	    islands.forEach((island, i) => {
   789	      const dock = harborPoint(island);
   790	      if (distance(world, dock) < 45) tappedHarbor = i;
   791	    });
   792	    if (tappedHarbor >= 0) { dockAt(tappedHarbor); return; }
   793	    if (p.y > H - 140) return;
   794	    player.targetHeading = Math.atan2(world.y - player.y, world.x - player.x);
   795	    addBurst(world.x, world.y, COLORS.foam, 5, .3);
   796	    setMessage('Course set', 1.2);
   797	  }

   799	  function handleMarketTap(x, y) {
   800	    const panel = { x: 12, y: Math.max(80, H * .12), w: W - 24, h: Math.min(H - Math.max(92, H * .12) - 12, 560) };
   801	    if (isInside(x, y, panel.x + panel.w - 72, panel.y + 8, 60, 56)) { marketOpen = false; activeHarbor = -1; return; }

   803	      const rowY = panel.y + 96 + i * 57;
   804	      if (isInside(x, y, panel.x + panel.w - 137, rowY, 62, 49)) buy(i);
   805	      if (isInside(x, y, panel.x + panel.w - 74, rowY, 62, 49)) sell(i);
   806	    });
   807	    const upgradeY = panel.y + 96 + GOODS.length * 57 + 18;
   808	    if (isInside(x, y, panel.x + panel.w - 145, upgradeY, 125, 55)) upgradeHull();
   809	  }

   811	  function keyDown(event) {
   812	    keys[event.key] = true;
   813	    if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', ' ', 'Enter', 'w', 'a', 's', 'd', 'W', 'A', 'S', 'D'].includes(event.key)) event.preventDefault();
   814	    if (event.key === 'r' || event.key === 'R') resetGame();
   815	    if (event.key === 'e' || event.key === 'E' || event.key === ' ' || event.key === 'Enter') {
   816	      if (marketOpen) { marketOpen = false; activeHarbor = -1; }
   817	      else if (!won) tryDock();
   818	    }
   819	  }

   822	  function loop(now) {
   823	    if (!lastFrame) lastFrame = now;
   824	    const dt = Math.min(.033, Math.max(0, (now - lastFrame) / 1000));
   825	    lastFrame = now;
   826	    update(dt);
   827	    draw();
   828	    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
   829	  }

    64	  function clamp(value, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value)); }
    65	  function lerp(a, b, t) { return a + (b - a) * t; }
    66	  function ease(t) { return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); }
    67	  function angleDelta(a, b) {
    68	    let d = (a - b + Math.PI) % TAU - Math.PI;
    69	    if (d < -Math.PI) d += TAU;
    70	    return d;
    71	  }
    72	  function formatTime(seconds) {
    73	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds)) return '--:--.--';
    74	    const mins = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
    75	    const secs = seconds - mins * 60;
    76	    return `${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${secs.toFixed(2).padStart(5, '0')}`;
    77	  }
    78	  function mulberry(seed) {
    79	    let value = seed >>> 0;
    80	    return () => {
    81	      value += 0x6D2B79F5;
    82	      let t = value;
    83	      t = Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 15, t | 1);
    84	      t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, t | 61);
    85	      return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    86	    };
    87	  }
    88	  function loadBest() {
    89	    try {
    90	      const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('rally-dust-best') || '{}');
    91	      return { stages: Array.isArray(saved.stages) ? saved.stages : [], total: Number(saved.total) || null };
    92	    } catch (error) {
    93	      return { stages: [], total: null };
    94	    }
    95	  }
    96	  function saveBest() {

   100	  function buildStage(index) {
   101	    const info = stageInfo[index];
   102	    const rng = mulberry(info.seed);
   103	    const count = 600 + index * 12;
   104	    const step = 28 + index * 1.2;
   105	    const events = [];
   106	    let at = 45;
   107	    let sign = rng() > .5 ? 1 : -1;
   108	    while (at < count - 40) {
   109	      const roll = rng();
   110	      const kind = roll < .13 ? 'jump' : roll < .25 ? 'rocks' : roll < .41 ? 'chicane' : roll < .59 ? 'hairpin' : 'turn';
   111	      if (kind === 'jump' || kind === 'rocks') {
   112	        events.push({ index: Math.round(at), kind, delta: 0, duration: 26, label: kind });
   113	      } else if (kind === 'chicane') {
   114	        sign *= -1;
   115	        const magnitude = .72 + rng() * .2;
   116	        events.push({ index: Math.round(at), kind, delta: sign * magnitude, duration: 23, label: 'chicane' });
   117	        events.push({ index: Math.round(at + 17), kind: 'turn', delta: sign * -1.12, duration: 23, visualOnly: true, label: 'chicane' });
   118	      } else {
   119	        sign *= -1;
   120	        const magnitude = kind === 'hairpin' ? 1.58 + rng() * .24 : .52 + rng() * .46;
   121	        events.push({ index: Math.round(at), kind, delta: sign * magnitude, duration: kind === 'hairpin' ? 39 : 25, tightens: kind === 'turn' && rng() > .54 });
   122	      }
   123	      at += 45 + Math.floor(rng() * 38);
   124	    }
   125	
   126	    const path = [];
   127	    let x = 0;
   128	    let y = 0;
   129	    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {
   130	      let angle = Math.sin(i * .017 + index * 1.7) * .08;
   131	      for (const event of events) {
   132	        const start = event.index - event.duration * .55;
   133	        const t = clamp((i - start) / event.duration, 0, 1);
   134	        if (t > 0) angle += event.delta * ease(t);
   135	      }
   136	      const crest = Math.sin(i * .042 + index * 2.1) * .12 + Math.sin(i * .013) * .09;
   137	      path.push({ x, y, heading: angle, altitude: crest, roadHalf: 177 + Math.sin(i * .025) * 5 });
   138	      x += Math.cos(angle) * step;
   139	      y += Math.sin(angle) * step;
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    const trees = [];
   143	    for (let i = 6; i < path.length - 5; i += 4) {
   144	      if (rng() > .23) {
   145	        const p = path[i];
   146	        const normalX = -Math.sin(p.heading);
   147	        const normalY = Math.cos(p.heading);
   148	        for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
   149	          if (rng() < .84) {
   150	            const distance = p.roadHalf + 80 + rng() * 220;
   151	            trees.push({ x: p.x + normalX * side * distance, y: p.y + normalY * side * distance, size: 17 + rng() * 22, hue: rng() });
   152	          }
   153	        }
   154	      }
   155	    }
   156	
   157	    const rocks = [];
   158	    for (const event of events) {
   159	      if (event.kind !== 'rocks' || !path[event.index]) continue;
   160	      const p = path[event.index];
   161	      const normalX = -Math.sin(p.heading);
   162	      const normalY = Math.cos(p.heading);
   163	      const side = rng() > .5 ? 1 : -1;
   164	      const distance = p.roadHalf * (.72 + rng() * .2);
   165	      rocks.push({ x: p.x + normalX * side * distance, y: p.y + normalY * side * distance, size: 12 + rng() * 12 });
   166	      rocks.push({ x: p.x + normalX * -side * (distance + 20), y: p.y + normalY * -side * (distance + 20), size: 7 + rng() * 8 });
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    const features = events.filter((event) => !event.visualOnly).sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);
   170	    return { info, path, trees, rocks, features, par: info.par + index * 2, step };
   171	  }

   173	  function featureText(feature) {
   174	    if (!feature) return '';
   175	    if (feature.kind === 'jump') return 'JUMP';
   176	    if (feature.kind === 'rocks') return 'CAUTION ROCKS';
   177	    if (feature.kind === 'chicane') return 'CAUTION CHICANE';
   178	    const direction = feature.delta < 0 ? 'LEFT' : 'RIGHT';
   179	    if (feature.kind === 'hairpin') return `${direction} HAIRPIN`;
   180	    const grade = Math.abs(feature.delta) > .85 ? '2' : Math.abs(feature.delta) > .65 ? '3' : '5';
   181	    return `${direction} ${grade}${feature.tightens ? ' TIGHTENS' : ''}`;
   182	  }

   218	  function nearestOnRoad() {
   219	    let bestIndex = car.nearest || 8;
   220	    let bestDistance = Infinity;
   221	    const start = clamp(bestIndex - 28, 0, stage.path.length - 1);
   222	    const end = clamp(bestIndex + 65, 0, stage.path.length - 1);
   223	    for (let i = start; i <= end; i += 1) {
   224	      const p = stage.path[i];
   225	      const dx = car.x - p.x;
   226	      const dy = car.y - p.y;
   227	      const distance = dx * dx + dy * dy;
   228	      if (distance < bestDistance) { bestDistance = distance; bestIndex = i; }
   229	    }
   230	    const p = stage.path[bestIndex];
   231	    const dx = car.x - p.x;
   232	    const dy = car.y - p.y;
   233	    const normalX = -Math.sin(p.heading);
   234	    const normalY = Math.cos(p.heading);
   235	    car.nearest = bestIndex;
   236	    car.lateral = dx * normalX + dy * normalY;
   237	    return p;
   238	  }

   240	  function controls() {
   241	    const left = keys.ArrowLeft || keys.KeyA;
   242	    const right = keys.ArrowRight || keys.KeyD;
   243	    const keyboardSteer = (right ? 1 : 0) - (left ? 1 : 0);
   244	    return {
   245	      steer: keyboardSteer || pointer.steer,
   246	      brake: !!(keys.ArrowDown || keys.KeyS || pointer.brake),
   247	      handbrake: !!(keys.Space || keys.Enter || pointer.handbrake)
   248	    };
   249	  }

   240	  function controls() {
   241	    const left = keys.ArrowLeft || keys.KeyA;
   242	    const right = keys.ArrowRight || keys.KeyD;
   243	    const keyboardSteer = (right ? 1 : 0) - (left ? 1 : 0);
   244	    return {
   245	      steer: keyboardSteer || pointer.steer,
   246	      brake: !!(keys.ArrowDown || keys.KeyS || pointer.brake),
   247	      handbrake: !!(keys.Space || keys.Enter || pointer.handbrake)
   248	    };
   249	  }
   250	
   251	  function update(dt) {
   252	    updateParticles(dt);
   253	    if (stageToastTime > 0) {
   254	      stageToastTime -= dt;
   255	      if (stageToastTime <= 0) stageToast.classList.remove('show');
   256	    }
   257	    if (hintTime > 0) {
   258	      hintTime -= dt;
   259	      if (hintTime <= 0) hint.classList.add('hide');
   260	    }
   261	    if (mode !== 'running' && mode !== 'reset') return;
   262	
   263	    stageTime += dt;
   264	    if (stageTime >= STAGE_LIMIT) {
   265	      stageTime = STAGE_LIMIT;
   266	      mode = 'failed';
   267	      stageToast.classList.remove('show');
   268	      showStageResult(false);
   269	      return;
   270	    }
   271	    if (mode === 'reset') {
   272	      car.resetTime -= dt;
   273	      if (car.resetTime <= 0) {
   274	        mode = 'running';
   275	        stageToastTime = 0;
   276	        stageToast.classList.remove('show');
   277	      }
   278	      updateCamera(dt);
   279	      return;
   280	    }
   281	
   282	    const input = controls();
   283	    const point = nearestOnRoad();
   284	    const forwardX = Math.cos(car.heading);
   285	    const forwardY = Math.sin(car.heading);
   286	    const sideX = -forwardY;
   287	    const sideY = forwardX;
   288	    let forwardSpeed = car.vx * forwardX + car.vy * forwardY;
   289	    let sideSpeed = car.vx * sideX + car.vy * sideY;
   290	    const maxSpeed = 325 - stageIndex * 5;
   291	
   292	    if (input.brake) forwardSpeed -= 420 * dt;
   293	    else forwardSpeed += (370 - forwardSpeed * .48) * dt;
   294	    forwardSpeed = clamp(forwardSpeed, 0, maxSpeed);
   295	    const grip = input.handbrake ? .53 : .79;
   296	    sideSpeed *= Math.pow(grip, dt * 60);
   297	    const turnPower = (.72 + clamp(forwardSpeed / maxSpeed, 0, 1) * 1.34) * (input.handbrake ? 2.15 : 1);
   298	    car.heading += input.steer * turnPower * dt;
   299	    if (!input.handbrake && Math.abs(car.lateral) < point.roadHalf * 1.15) {
   300	      car.heading += angleDelta(point.heading, car.heading) * dt * (.2 + forwardSpeed / maxSpeed * .12);
   301	    }
   302	    const newForwardX = Math.cos(car.heading);
   303	    const newForwardY = Math.sin(car.heading);
   304	    const newSideX = -newForwardY;
   305	    const newSideY = newForwardX;
   306	    car.vx = newForwardX * forwardSpeed + newSideX * sideSpeed;
   307	    car.vy = newForwardY * forwardSpeed + newSideY * sideSpeed;
   308	    car.x += car.vx * dt;
   309	    car.y += car.vy * dt;
   310	    car.speed = Math.max(0, forwardSpeed);
   311	
   312	    const updatedPoint = nearestOnRoad();
   313	    const outside = Math.abs(car.lateral) - updatedPoint.roadHalf;
   314	    if (outside > 0) {
   315	      car.offroad += dt * (outside > 75 ? 1.9 : .45);
   316	      if (outside > 112) shake = Math.max(shake, 2);
   317	    } else {
   318	      car.offroad = Math.max(0, car.offroad - dt * 2.8);
   319	    }
   320	    if (car.offroad > .72) {
   321	      triggerReset();
   322	      return;
   323	    }
   324	
   325	    const driftAmount = Math.abs(sideSpeed);
   326	    if (driftAmount > 35 && car.speed > 90) {
   327	      car.drift = clamp(car.drift + dt * driftAmount * .014, 0, 99);
   328	      skidMarks.push({ x: car.x - newForwardX * 20, y: car.y - newForwardY * 20, heading: car.heading, life: 1 });
   329	      if (skidMarks.length > 90) skidMarks.shift();
   330	      shake = Math.max(shake, input.handbrake ? 2.2 : .5);
   331	    }
   332	    if (car.speed > 85 && Math.random() < dt * 18) spawnDust(car.x - newForwardX * 25, car.y - newForwardY * 25, input.handbrake ? 1.5 : 1);
   333	    car.bounce = updatedPoint.altitude * .4;
   334	    updateCamera(dt);
   335	
   336	    if (car.nearest >= stage.path.length - 14) {
   337	      mode = 'complete';
   338	      stageToast.classList.remove('show');
   339	      recordStageTime();
   340	      showStageResult(true);
   341	    }
   342	  }
   343	
   344	  function triggerReset() {
   345	    const target = stage.path[clamp(car.nearest - 18, 8, stage.path.length - 1)];
   346	    car.x = target.x;
   347	    car.y = target.y;
   348	    car.heading = target.heading;
   349	    car.vx = 0;
   350	    car.vy = 0;
   351	    car.speed = 0;
   352	    car.nearest = clamp(car.nearest - 18, 8, stage.path.length - 1);
   353	    car.lateral = 0;
   354	    car.offroad = 0;
   355	    car.resetTime = 3;
   356	    mode = 'reset';
   357	    shake = 8;

   360	    stageToast.querySelector('strong').textContent = '+3 SEC RESET';

   362	    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i += 1) spawnDust(car.x, car.y, 2.5);
   363	  }
   364	
   365	  function recordStageTime() {
   366	    const old = Number(bestData.stages[stageIndex]);
   367	    stageWasNewBest = !old || stageTime < old;
   368	    if (!old || stageTime < old) {
   369	      bestData.stages[stageIndex] = stageTime;
   370	      saveBest();
   371	    }
   372	  }
   373	
   374	  function showStageResult(success) {
   375	    screen.classList.add('show');
   376	    screenMedal.textContent = '';
   377	    resultRows.innerHTML = '';
   378	    stagePicker?.classList.remove('show');
   379	    if (!success) {
   380	      screenOverline.textContent = 'STAGE TIMEOUT';
   381	      screenTitle.textContent = 'DUSTED OUT';
   382	      screenCopy.textContent = 'The clock won this run. Reset the line and attack the next attempt.';
   383	      resultRows.innerHTML = `<div class="result-row"><span>Stage time</span><strong>${formatTime(stageTime)} / 01:30.00</strong></div>`;
   384	      retryButton.textContent = 'RETRY STAGE';
   385	      nextButton.textContent = 'RETRY STAGE';
   386	      return;
   387	    }
   388	    const newBest = stageWasNewBest;
   389	    runTimes[stageIndex] = stageTime;
   390	    const rallyTotal = stageTimesThrough(stageIndex);
   391	    if (stageIndex === stageInfo.length - 1) {
   392	      bestData.total = !bestData.total || rallyTotal < bestData.total ? rallyTotal : bestData.total;
   393	      saveBest();
   394	      const medal = medalFor(rallyTotal);
   395	      screenOverline.textContent = 'RALLY COMPLETE';
   396	      screenTitle.textContent = 'THE DUST IS YOURS';
   397	      screenCopy.textContent = `Five stages. One total. ${medal === 'GOLD' ? 'That was a champion line.' : 'There is more time in the corners.'}`;
   398	      resultRows.innerHTML = stageInfo.map((info, i) => `<div class="result-row"><span>Stage ${i + 1} · ${info.name}</span><strong>${formatTime(runTimes[i])}</strong></div>`).join('') + `<div class="result-row"><span>Rally total</span><strong>${formatTime(rallyTotal)}</strong></div>`;
   399	      screenMedal.textContent = `${medal} MEDAL`;

   401	      nextButton.textContent = 'REPLAY RALLY';

   405	      }

   411	      retryButton.textContent = 'RETRY STAGE';
   412	      nextButton.textContent = 'NEXT STAGE';
   413	    }
   414	    saveBest();
   415	  }
   416	
   417	  function stageTimesThrough(lastIndex) {
   418	    let total = 0;
   419	    for (let i = 0; i <= lastIndex; i += 1) total += Number(runTimes[i]) || (i === stageIndex ? stageTime : stageInfo[i].par);
   420	    return total;
   421	  }
   422	
   423	  function medalFor(total) {
   424	    const par = stageInfo.reduce((sum, info) => sum + info.par, 0);
   425	    if (total <= par * 1.06) return 'GOLD';
   426	    if (total <= par * 1.2) return 'SILVER';
   427	    return 'BRONZE';
   428	  }
   429	
   430	  function updateCamera(dt) {
   431	    if (!stage) return;
   432	    const lead = 175;
   433	    const targetX = car.x + Math.cos(car.heading) * lead;
   434	    const targetY = car.y + Math.sin(car.heading) * lead;
   435	    const follow = 1 - Math.pow(.0005, dt);
   436	    camera.x = lerp(camera.x, targetX, follow);
   437	    camera.y = lerp(camera.y, targetY, follow);
   438	    shake = Math.max(0, shake - dt * 10);
   439	  }
   440	
   441	  function spawnDust(x, y, force) {
   442	    if (particles.length > 150) particles.shift();
   443	    const angle = Math.random() * TAU;
   444	    particles.push({ x, y, vx: Math.cos(angle) * (12 + Math.random() * 25) * force, vy: Math.sin(angle) * (12 + Math.random() * 25) * force, life: .45 + Math.random() * .4, max: .8, size: 4 + Math.random() * 9 * force });
   445	  }
   446	  function updateParticles(dt) {
   447	    for (let i = particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   448	      const particle = particles[i];
   449	      particle.life -= dt;
   450	      particle.x += particle.vx * dt;
   451	      particle.y += particle.vy * dt;
   452	      particle.vx *= .96;
   453	      particle.vy *= .96;
   454	      if (particle.life <= 0) particles.splice(i, 1);
   455	    }
   456	    for (let i = skidMarks.length - 1; i >= 0; i -= 1) {
   457	      skidMarks[i].life -= dt * .12;
   458	      if (skidMarks[i].life <= 0) skidMarks.splice(i, 1);
   459	    }
   460	  }
   461	
   462	  function resize() {
   463	    view.w = window.innerWidth;
   464	    view.h = window.innerHeight;
   465	    view.dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
   466	    const longAxis = Math.max(view.w, view.h);
   467	    view.scale = Math.min(view.dpr, 960 / Math.max(longAxis, 1));
   468	    canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(view.w * view.scale));
   469	    canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(view.h * view.scale));
   470	    canvas.style.width = `${view.w}px`;
   471	    canvas.style.height = `${view.h}px`;
   472	    ctx.setTransform(view.scale, 0, 0, view.scale, 0, 0);
   473	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
   474	  }
   475	
   476	  function screenPoint(x, y) {
   477	    const zoom = clamp(Math.min(view.w / 920, view.h / 650), .48, .88);
   478	    const sx = (x - camera.x) * zoom + view.w * .5 + view.shakeX;
   479	    const sy = (y - camera.y) * zoom + view.h * .55 + view.shakeY;
   480	    return { x: sx, y: sy, zoom };
   481	  }
   482	
   483	  function draw() {
   484	    if (!stage) return;
   485	    view.shakeX = (Math.random() - .5) * shake;
   486	    view.shakeY = (Math.random() - .5) * shake;
   487	    ctx.save();
   488	    ctx.setTransform(view.scale, 0, 0, view.scale, 0, 0);
   489	    const gradient = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, view.h);

   492	    gradient.addColorStop(1, '#15271e');

   662	  function pointerDown(event) {
   663	    if (event.target.closest('.pedal') || event.target.closest('#screen')) return;
   664	    if (event.clientX > view.w * .55) return;
   665	    event.preventDefault();
   666	    pointer.id = event.pointerId;
   667	    pointer.startX = event.clientX;
   668	    pointer.steer = 0;
   669	    hintTime = 0;
   670	    hint.classList.add('hide');
   671	    game.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   672	  }
   673	  function pointerMove(event) {
   674	    if (pointer.id !== event.pointerId) return;
   675	    event.preventDefault();
   676	    pointer.steer = clamp((event.clientX - pointer.startX) / Math.max(80, view.w * .12), -1, 1);
   677	  }
   678	  function pointerUp(event) {
   679	    if (pointer.id !== event.pointerId) return;
   680	    pointer.id = null;
   681	    pointer.steer = 0;
   682	  }
   683	
   684	  function bindHoldButton(element, property) {
   685	    element.addEventListener('pointerdown', (event) => {
   686	      event.preventDefault();
   687	      pointer[property] = true;
   688	      element.classList.add('active');
   689	      element.setPointerCapture?.(event.pointerId);
   690	    });
   691	    const release = (event) => {
   692	      event.preventDefault();
   693	      pointer[property] = false;
   694	      element.classList.remove('active');
   695	    };
   696	    element.addEventListener('pointerup', release);
   697	    element.addEventListener('pointercancel', release);
   698	    element.addEventListener('pointerleave', (event) => { if (event.buttons === 0) release(event); });
   699	  }
   700	
   701	  function onKey(event, down) {
   702	    keys[event.code] = down;
   703	    if (['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'Space', 'Enter', 'KeyA', 'KeyD', 'KeyS', 'KeyW'].includes(event.code)) event.preventDefault();
   704	    if (down && !event.repeat && event.code === 'Enter' && mode !== 'running' && mode !== 'reset') advanceFromScreen();
   705	    if (down && hintTime > 0) { hintTime = 0; hint.classList.add('hide'); }
   706	  }
   707	
   708	  function advanceFromScreen() {
   709	    if (stageIndex === stageInfo.length - 1 && mode === 'complete') beginStage(0);
   710	    else beginStage(stageIndex + (mode === 'complete' ? 1 : 0));
   711	  }
   712	
   713	  function bindUi() {
   714	    window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
   715	    window.addEventListener('keydown', (event) => onKey(event, true), { passive: false });
   716	    window.addEventListener('keyup', (event) => onKey(event, false), { passive: false });
   717	    game.addEventListener('pointerdown', pointerDown, { passive: false });
   718	    game.addEventListener('pointermove', pointerMove, { passive: false });
   719	    game.addEventListener('pointerup', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   720	    game.addEventListener('pointercancel', pointerUp, { passive: false });
   721	    game.addEventListener('touchmove', (event) => event.preventDefault(), { passive: false });
   722	    bindHoldButton(handbrakeButton, 'handbrake');
   723	    bindHoldButton(brakeButton, 'brake');
   724	    retryButton.addEventListener('click', () => beginStage(stageIndex));
   725	    nextButton.addEventListener('click', advanceFromScreen);
   726	    if (stagePicker) stagePicker.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
   727	      const button = event.target.closest('[data-stage]');
   728	      if (button) beginStage(Number(button.dataset.stage));
   729	    });
   730	  }

   732	  function frame(now) {
   733	    const dt = Math.min(.05, Math.max(.001, (now - lastFrame) / 1000));
   734	    lastFrame = now;
   735	    update(dt);
   736	    draw();
   737	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   738	  }

    37	    banner: null, bannerT: 0,
    38	    best: 0,
    39	    winner: -1,
    40	    t0: 0,
    41	    hits: [],
    42	    pressed: null, pressT: 0,
    43	    started: false
    44	  };
    45	
    46	  try { S.best = parseInt(localStorage.getItem(BEST_KEY) || '0', 10) || 0; } catch (e) { S.best = 0; }
    47	
    48	  // ---------- sizing ----------
    49	  function resize() {
    50	    var cssW = window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    51	    var cssH = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight;
    52	    W = Math.max(240, cssW); H = Math.max(360, cssH);
    53	    var dpr = Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
    54	    var longSide = Math.max(W, H);
    55	    sc = Math.min(dpr, 960 / longSide);
    56	    cv.width = Math.round(W * sc);
    57	    cv.height = Math.round(H * sc);
    58	    cv.style.width = W + 'px';
    59	    cv.style.height = H + 'px';
    60	  }
    61	  window.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    62	  window.addEventListener('orientationchange', function () { setTimeout(resize, 120); });
    63	  resize();
    64	
    65	  // ---------- deck ----------
    66	  function buildDeck() {
    67	    var d = [];
    68	    for (var c = 0; c < DECK.length; c++) {
    69	      var cat = DECK[c];
    70	      for (var i = 0; i < cat[2].length; i++) d.push({ text: cat[2][i], cat: cat[0], col: cat[1] });
    71	    }
    72	    for (var j = d.length - 1; j > 0; j--) {
    73	      var k = (Math.random() * (j + 1)) | 0, t = d[j]; d[j] = d[k]; d[k] = t;
    74	    }
    75	    S.deck = d; S.di = 0;
    76	  }
    77	  function nextPhrase() {
    78	    if (S.di >= S.deck.length) {
    79	      var prev = S.cur;
    80	      buildDeck();
    81	      // never repeat the just-shown phrase across a reshuffle
    82	      if (prev && S.deck.length > 1 && S.deck[0].text === prev.text) {
    83	        var sw = S.deck[0]; S.deck[0] = S.deck[1]; S.deck[1] = sw;
    84	      }
    85	    }
    86	    S.cur = S.deck[S.di++];
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  // ---------- game flow ----------
    90	  function newGame() {
    91	    buildDeck();
    92	    S.roundPts = [0, 0]; S.gotPts = [0, 0]; S.history = [];
    93	    S.roundNo = 1; S.winner = -1;
    94	    S.holder = (Math.random() < 0.5) ? 0 : 1;
    95	    startRound();
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  function startRound() {
    99	    S.roundGot = [0, 0];
   100	    S.dur = MIN_T + Math.random() * (MAX_T - MIN_T);
   101	    S.left = S.dur;
   102	    S.tickAcc = 0; S.pulse = 0; S.handoff = 0;
   103	    nextPhrase();
   104	    S.scene = 'play';
   105	    banner(TEAMS[S.holder].name + ' STARTS', TEAMS[S.holder].col, 1.3);
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  function gotIt() {
   109	    S.gotPts[S.holder]++; S.roundGot[S.holder]++;
   110	    var b = Math.max(S.gotPts[0], S.gotPts[1]);
   111	    if (b > S.best) { S.best = b; try { localStorage.setItem(BEST_KEY, String(b)); } catch (e) {} }
   112	    SND.got();
   113	    burst(W * 0.5, H * 0.42, 26, '#7ef29a');
   114	    S.shake = 7; S.flash = 0.35; S.flashCol = '#7ef29a';
   115	    S.holder = 1 - S.holder;
   116	    S.handoff = 1.0;
   117	    SND.handoff();
   118	    nextPhrase();
   119	  }
   120	
   121	  function passIt() {
   122	    SND.pass();
   123	    S.shake = 3; S.flash = 0.18; S.flashCol = '#8a93a8';
   124	    burst(W * 0.5, H * 0.42, 10, '#8a93a8');
   125	    nextPhrase();
   126	  }
   127	
   128	  function endRound() {
   129	    var loser = S.holder, winr = 1 - S.holder;
   130	    S.roundPts[winr]++;
   131	    S.history.push({ n: S.roundNo, w: winr, l: loser, g: [S.roundGot[0], S.roundGot[1]] });
   132	    SND.buzz();
   133	    S.shake = 30; S.flash = 0.8; S.flashCol = '#ff3b30';
   134	    burst(W * 0.5, H * 0.45, 70, '#ff5540');
   135	    S.holder = winr;            // phone passes to the other team
   136	    if (S.roundPts[winr] >= TARGET) {
   137	      S.winner = winr;
   138	      S.scene = 'win';
   139	      setTimeout(function () { SND.win(); }, 620);
   140	    } else {
   141	      S.scene = 'roundover';
   142	    }
   143	  }
   144	
   145	  function advance() {
   146	    S.roundNo++;
   147	    startRound();
   148	  }
   149	
   150	  // ---------- juice ----------
   151	  function burst(x, y, n, col) {
   152	    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
   153	      var a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, sp = 60 + Math.random() * 340;
   154	      S.parts.push({
   155	        x: x, y: y, vx: Math.cos(a) * sp, vy: Math.sin(a) * sp - 40,
   156	        life: 0.5 + Math.random() * 0.7, max: 1, r: 2 + Math.random() * 4, c: col
   157	      });
   158	    }
   159	    if (S.parts.length > 260) S.parts.splice(0, S.parts.length - 260);
   160	  }
   161	  function banner(txt, col, t) { S.banner = { t: txt, c: col }; S.bannerT = t; }
   162	
   163	  // ---------- input ----------
   164	  function hit(id, x, y, w, h) { S.hits.push({ id: id, x: x, y: y, w: w, h: h }); }
   165	
   166	  function pick(px, py) {
   167	    for (var i = S.hits.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   168	      var r = S.hits[i];
   169	      if (px >= r.x && px <= r.x + r.w && py >= r.y && py <= r.y + r.h) return r.id;
   170	    }
   171	    return null;
   172	  }
   173	
   174	  function act(id) {
   175	    SND.init();
   176	    if (!S.started) { S.started = true; }
   177	    if (S.scene === 'title') {
   178	      if (id === 'mute') { SND.toggleMute(); return; }
   179	      SND.tap(); newGame(); return;
   180	    }
   181	    if (S.scene === 'play') {
   182	      if (id === 'mute') { SND.toggleMute(); return; }
   183	      if (S.handoff > 0.5) return;        // brief lockout while handing over
   184	      if (id === 'got') gotIt();
   185	      else if (id === 'pass') passIt();
   186	      return;
   187	    }
   188	    if (S.scene === 'roundover') { if (id === 'mute') { SND.toggleMute(); return; } SND.tap(); advance(); return; }
   189	    if (S.scene === 'win') {
   190	      if (id === 'mute') { SND.toggleMute(); return; }
   191	      SND.tap(); newGame(); return;
   192	    }
   193	  }
   194	
   195	  function down(px, py) {
   196	    var id = pick(px, py);
   197	    S.pressed = id; S.pressT = 0.12;
   198	    act(id);
   199	  }
   200	
   201	  var touching = false;
   202	  cv.addEventListener('touchstart', function (e) {
   203	    touching = true;
   204	    e.preventDefault();
   205	    var t = e.changedTouches[0], r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   206	    down(t.clientX - r.left, t.clientY - r.top);
   207	  }, { passive: false });
   208	  cv.addEventListener('touchmove', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   209	  cv.addEventListener('touchend', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   210	  cv.addEventListener('touchcancel', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); }, { passive: false });
   211	
   212	  cv.addEventListener('mousedown', function (e) {
   213	    if (touching) return;
   214	    var r = cv.getBoundingClientRect();
   215	    down(e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top);
   216	  });
   217	
   218	  document.addEventListener('contextmenu', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   219	  document.addEventListener('gesturestart', function (e) { e.preventDefault(); });
   220	
   221	  window.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
   222	    var k = e.key;
   223	    if (k === ' ' || k === 'Enter' || k === 'ArrowRight' || k === 'ArrowUp' ||
   224	        k === 'd' || k === 'D' || k === 'w' || k === 'W') {
   225	      e.preventDefault();
   226	      if (S.scene === 'play') { S.pressed = 'got'; S.pressT = 0.12; act('got'); }
   227	      else act('go');
   228	    } else if (k === 'ArrowLeft' || k === 'ArrowDown' || k === 'a' || k === 'A' ||
   229	               k === 's' || k === 'S' || k === 'p' || k === 'P' || k === 'Backspace') {
   230	      e.preventDefault();
   231	      if (S.scene === 'play') { S.pressed = 'pass'; S.pressT = 0.12; act('pass'); }
   232	      else act('go');
   233	    } else if (k === 'r' || k === 'R') {
   234	      e.preventDefault(); SND.init(); newGame();
   235	    } else if (k === 'm' || k === 'M') {
   236	      e.preventDefault(); SND.init(); SND.toggleMute();
   237	    }
   238	  });

   240	  // ---------- draw helpers ----------
   241	  function rr(x, y, w, h, r) {
   242	    r = Math.min(r, w * 0.5, h * 0.5);
   243	    ctx.beginPath();
   244	    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
   245	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
   246	    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
   247	    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
   248	    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
   249	    ctx.closePath();
   250	  }
   251	  function txt(s, x, y, size, col, weight, align, base) {
   252	    ctx.font = (weight || '700') + ' ' + size + 'px ' + FONT;
   253	    ctx.fillStyle = col;
   254	    ctx.textAlign = align || 'center';
   255	    ctx.textBaseline = base || 'middle';
   256	    ctx.fillText(s, x, y);
   257	  }
   258	  function wrap(s, maxW) {
   259	    var words = s.split(' '), lines = [], line = '';
   260	    for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {

exec
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba shout-it/game.js | sed -n '260,680p'" in /Users/lucille/greenguard-usa-web/play
 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	    for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
   261	      var t = line ? line + ' ' + words[i] : words[i];
   262	      if (ctx.measureText(t).width > maxW && line) { lines.push(line); line = words[i]; }
   263	      else line = t;
   264	    }
   265	    if (line) lines.push(line);
   266	    return lines;
   267	  }
   268	  function fitLines(s, maxW, maxH, hi, lo, weight, lh) {
   269	    for (var size = hi; size >= lo; size -= 2) {
   270	      ctx.font = (weight || '800') + ' ' + size + 'px ' + FONT;
   271	      var ls = wrap(s, maxW);
   272	      if (ls.length * size * lh <= maxH) return { size: size, lines: ls };
   273	    }
   274	    ctx.font = (weight || '800') + ' ' + lo + 'px ' + FONT;
   275	    return { size: lo, lines: wrap(s, maxW) };
   276	  }

   278	  // ---------- layout ----------
   279	  function layout() {
   280	    var pad = Math.round(W * 0.04);
   281	    var btnH = Math.max(88, Math.min(128, Math.round(H * 0.155)));
   282	    var botPad = Math.round(Math.min(26, H * 0.028)) + 8;
   283	    var btnY = H - botPad - btnH;
   284	    var barY = Math.round(Math.min(18, H * 0.022));
   285	    var barH = Math.round(Math.min(66, H * 0.088));
   286	    var bannerY = barY + barH + 8;
   287	    var bannerH = 28;
   288	    var cardY = bannerY + bannerH + 10;
   289	    var cardH = btnY - 14 - cardY;
   290	    return { pad: pad, btnH: btnH, btnY: btnY, barY: barY, barH: barH,
   291	             bannerY: bannerY, bannerH: bannerH, cardY: cardY, cardH: cardH };
   292	  }
   293	
   294	  // ---------- update ----------
   295	  function update(dt) {
   296	    if (S.shake > 0) { S.shake = Math.max(0, S.shake - dt * 60); }
   297	    if (S.flash > 0) S.flash = Math.max(0, S.flash - dt * 2.6);
   298	    if (S.pulse > 0) S.pulse = Math.max(0, S.pulse - dt * 3.4);
   299	    if (S.bannerT > 0) S.bannerT = Math.max(0, S.bannerT - dt);
   300	    if (S.pressT > 0) { S.pressT -= dt; if (S.pressT <= 0) S.pressed = null; }
   301	
   302	    for (var i = S.parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
   303	      var p = S.parts[i];
   304	      p.life -= dt;
   305	      if (p.life <= 0) { S.parts.splice(i, 1); continue; }
   306	      p.vy += 900 * dt;
   307	      p.vx *= (1 - 1.4 * dt);
   308	      p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
   309	    }
   310	
   311	    if (S.scene === 'play') {
   312	      S.left -= dt;
   313	      if (S.handoff > 0) S.handoff = Math.max(0, S.handoff - dt);
   314	      var pr = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - S.left / S.dur));
   315	      S.tickAcc += dt;
   316	      var iv = 1.10 * Math.pow(0.085, pr);
   317	      if (S.tickAcc >= iv) { S.tickAcc = 0; SND.tick(pr); S.pulse = 1; }
   318	      if (S.left <= 0) endRound();
   319	    }
   320	  }
   321	
   322	  // ---------- scenes ----------
   323	  function drawBG(heat) {
   324	    var g = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
   325	    g.addColorStop(0, '#131829');
   326	    g.addColorStop(1, '#0b0e16');
   327	    ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   328	    if (heat > 0) {
   329	      var rg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W * 0.5, H * 0.5, Math.min(W, H) * 0.25,
   330	                                        W * 0.5, H * 0.5, Math.max(W, H) * 0.72);
   331	      rg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,60,40,0)');
   332	      rg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,45,30,' + (0.06 + 0.5 * heat * heat).toFixed(3) + ')');
   333	      ctx.fillStyle = rg; ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
   334	    }
   335	  }
   336	
   337	  function drawScoreBar(L) {
   338	    var w = (W - L.pad * 2 - 10) / 2;
   339	    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
   340	      var x = L.pad + i * (w + 10), T = TEAMS[i];
   341	      var active = (S.scene === 'play' && S.holder === i);
   342	      ctx.save();
   343	      if (active) {
   344	        ctx.shadowColor = T.glow + '0.85)';
   345	        ctx.shadowBlur = 16 + 10 * S.pulse;
   346	      }
   347	      rr(x, L.barY, w, L.barH, 12);
   348	      ctx.fillStyle = active ? T.col : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)';
   349	      ctx.fill();
   350	      ctx.restore();
   351	      rr(x, L.barY, w, L.barH, 12);
   352	      ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = active ? '#ffffff' : T.dim; ctx.stroke();
   353	
   354	      var fg = active ? '#10131c' : T.col;
   355	      txt(T.name, x + 12, L.barY + L.barH * 0.32, Math.min(15, w * 0.16), fg, '800', 'left');
   356	      txt(String(S.roundPts[i]) + '/' + TARGET, x + w - 12, L.barY + L.barH * 0.32,
   357	          Math.min(17, w * 0.18), fg, '900', 'right');
   358	      // pip row
   359	      var pw = (w - 24) / TARGET, py = L.barY + L.barH * 0.72;
   360	      for (var k = 0; k < TARGET; k++) {
   361	        var px = x + 12 + k * pw;
   362	        rr(px, py - 3, pw - 3, 6, 3);
   363	        ctx.fillStyle = k < S.roundPts[i] ? (active ? '#10131c' : T.col) : (active ? 'rgba(0,0,0,0.22)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)');
   364	        ctx.fill();
   365	      }
   366	    }
   367	  }

   369	  function drawButtons(L) {

   372	    var gw = W - L.pad * 2 - gap - pw;

   380	    ctx.fill();
   381	    rr(px, py + pd, pw, L.btnH - pd, 18);
   382	    ctx.lineWidth = 2; ctx.strokeStyle = locked ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.10)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)'; ctx.stroke();
   383	    txt('PASS', px + pw / 2, py + pd + (L.btnH - pd) / 2 - 6, Math.min(26, pw * 0.24),
   384	        locked ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)' : '#e8ecf5', '900');
   385	    txt('no point', px + pw / 2, py + pd + (L.btnH - pd) / 2 + 18, 12,
   386	        locked ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)' : 'rgba(232,236,245,0.5)', '600');
   387	    hit('pass', px, py - 6, pw, L.btnH + 6);
   388	
   389	    // GOT IT
   390	    var gx = L.pad + pw + gap;
   391	    var gd = S.pressed === 'got' ? 3 : 0;
   392	    ctx.save();
   393	    if (!locked) { ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(96,240,140,0.45)'; ctx.shadowBlur = 18; }
   394	    rr(gx, py + gd, gw, L.btnH - gd, 18);

   398	    ctx.fillStyle = gg; ctx.fill();

   401	        locked ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.28)' : '#0b2417', '900');
   402	    txt('point + hand it over', gx + gw / 2, py + gd + (L.btnH - gd) / 2 + 20, 12,
   403	        locked ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)' : 'rgba(11,36,23,0.62)', '700');
   404	    hit('got', gx, py - 6, gw, L.btnH + 6);
   405	  }

   407	  function drawMute(L) {
   408	    var s = 34, x = W - s - 6, y = H - s - 6;
   409	    hit('mute', x - 6, y - 6, s + 12, s + 12);
   410	    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
   411	    txt(SND.isMuted() ? 'MUTED' : 'SOUND', x + s / 2, y + s / 2, 10, '#fff', '700');
   412	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   413	  }

   415	  function drawPlay(L) {
   416	    var pr = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - S.left / S.dur));
   417	    drawBG(pr);
   418	    drawScoreBar(L);
   419	
   420	    var T = TEAMS[S.holder];
   421	
   422	    // holder banner line
   423	    if (S.bannerT > 0 && S.banner) {
   424	      txt(S.banner.t, W / 2, L.bannerY + L.bannerH / 2, 15, S.banner.c, '800');
   425	    } else {
   426	      txt(T.name + ' HAS THE PHONE', W / 2, L.bannerY + L.bannerH / 2, 14, T.col, '800');
   427	    }
   428	
   429	    // card
   430	    var cx = L.pad, cy = L.cardY, cw = W - L.pad * 2, ch = L.cardH;
   431	    ctx.save();

   433	    ctx.shadowBlur = 22 + 18 * S.pulse;
   434	    rr(cx, cy, cw, ch, 22);
   435	    var cg = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, cy, 0, cy + ch);
   436	    cg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.09)');
   437	    cg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.03)');

   442	    ctx.strokeStyle = T.col; ctx.stroke();

   444	    if (S.handoff > 0) {
   445	      // handoff curtain: hide phrase while the phone changes hands
   446	      var a = Math.min(1, S.handoff / 0.35);
   447	      ctx.save();
   448	      rr(cx, cy, cw, ch, 22); ctx.clip();
   449	      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(10,13,20,' + (0.86 * a).toFixed(2) + ')';

   454	      txt(T.name, W / 2, cy + ch * 0.53, f.size, T.col, '900');
   455	      txt('clock is still running', W / 2, cy + ch * 0.66, 13, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)', '600');
   456	      ctx.restore();
   457	    } else {
   458	      // category chip

   596	  }
   597	
   598	  function drawParts() {
   599	    for (var i = 0; i < S.parts.length; i++) {
   600	      var p = S.parts[i];
   601	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, p.life / 0.6));
   602	      ctx.fillStyle = p.c;
   603	      ctx.fillRect(p.x - p.r / 2, p.y - p.r / 2, p.r, p.r);
   604	    }
   605	    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   606	  }
   607	
   608	  // ---------- frame ----------
   609	  var last = 0;
   610	  function frame(t) {
   611	    if (!last) last = t;
   612	    var dt = Math.min(0.05, (t - last) / 1000);
   613	    last = t;
   614	    update(dt);
   615	
   616	    var L = layout();
   617	    S.hits.length = 0;
   618	
   619	    ctx.setTransform(sc, 0, 0, sc, 0, 0);
   620	    var sh = S.shake;
   621	    if (sh > 0.2) {
   622	      var a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, m = sh * 0.55;
   623	      ctx.translate(Math.cos(a) * m, Math.sin(a) * m);
   624	    }
   625	
   626	    if (S.scene === 'title') drawTitle(L);
   627	    else if (S.scene === 'play') drawPlay(L);
   628	    else if (S.scene === 'roundover') drawRoundOver(L);
   629	    else drawWin(L);
   630	
   631	    drawParts();
   632	
   633	    if (S.flash > 0.01) {
   634	      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(0.5, S.flash * 0.45);
   635	      ctx.fillStyle = S.flashCol;
   636	      ctx.fillRect(-20, -20, W + 40, H + 40);
   637	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
   638	    }
   639	
   640	    requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   641	  }
   642	
   643	  buildDeck();
   644	  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
   645	})();
